각 파일은 `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 30 20">…</svg>` 한 줄. `CREDITS.md`: "World War 1936 dormant-country and civil-war flags (resources/flags/ww_*.svg): simple shapes drawn for this fork, CC BY-SA 4.0."

- [ ] **Step 5: 순수 규칙 `packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/EndgameRules.ts`** (spec §6.2~§6.3, §8.3~§8.6, §9.1~§9.2, §12.1, §13.2~§13.3, §15.2, §3.5, §25.3)

```ts
import type { CountryTag } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import {
  OccupationLaw, SubjectLevel, WW_CIVIL_SHARE_MAX_BP, WW_CIVIL_SHARE_MIN_BP, WW_LL_MAX_CONTRACTS,
  WW_LL_TENSION_DEMOCRACY_PCT, WW_LL_TENSION_RECEIVER_PCT, WW_OCC_COMP_BASE_BP, WW_OCC_COMP_CAP_FIGHTING_BP,
  WW_OCC_RES_BASE_BP, WW_OCC_RES_BASE_FIGHTING_BP, WW_OCC_RES_DOWN_MAX_BP, WW_OCC_RES_UP_MAX_BP,
  WW_PEACE_POOL_PCT, WW_VOLUNTEER_MAX_DIV, WW_VOLUNTEER_MIN_DIV, WW_VOLUNTEER_TENSION_PCT,
  WW_INTEGRATE_COMP_BP, WW_INTEGRATE_DAYS, WW_OCC_LAW_COST_PP,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/EndgameTypes";
import type { WwOrderBlock } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/EconomyTypes";
import type { BattalionId, SupportId, TemplateDef } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/LandTypes";
import type { Ideology } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/PoliticsTypes";

// ---- Peace conference (spec §6.2, §6.3) ----

export function regionBaseValue(vp: number, factories: number, tiles: number): number {
  return 4 + 2 * vp + 2 * factories + Math.floor(tiles / 2000);
}

export interface CostMods {
  controls: boolean;
  core: boolean;
  claim: boolean;
  conquerGoal: boolean;
  otherControls: boolean;
}
export const COST_FACTORS: readonly (readonly [keyof CostMods, number])[] = [
  ["controls", 50], ["core", 50], ["claim", 75], ["conquerGoal", 90], ["otherControls", 200],
];
/** Plan decision 13: multiply every factor, divide once; at least 1. */
export function demandCost(base: number, m: CostMods): number {
  let num = base;
  let den = 1;
  for (const [k, f] of COST_FACTORS) {
    if (!m[k]) continue;
    num *= f;
    den *= 100;
  }
  return Math.max(1, Math.floor(num / den));
}

/** Country-wide and grouped demands: a percent of the summed region costs (spec §6.4). */
export const AGG_PCT = {
  annex_rest: 90, occupation_zone: 30, puppet: 50, release: 60, liberate: 30,
  change_government: 30, change_government_topple: 15, demilitarise: 20, disarm: 15,
} as const;
export function aggregateCost(costs: readonly number[], pct: number): number {
  let s = 0;
  for (const c of costs) s += c;
  return Math.max(1, Math.floor((s * pct) / 100));
}
export function dismantleCost(members: number): number {
  return Math.max(1, members * 20);
}
export function reparationsCost(k: number): number {
  return 12 * k;
}
export function takeNavyCost(weight: number): number {
  return Math.max(1, 2 * weight);
}
/** Spec §6.4: at most a quarter of the loser's civilian factories. */
export function maxReparations(lossCivs: number): number {
  return Math.floor(lossCivs / 4);
}

/** Spec §6.2, plan decision 12. */
export function participantPoints(totalBase: number, wsMilli: readonly number[]): number[] {
  const pool = Math.floor((totalBase * WW_PEACE_POOL_PCT) / 100);
  const n = wsMilli.length;
  if (n === 0) return [];
  let sum = 0;
  for (const w of wsMilli) sum += Math.max(0, w);
  if (sum === 0) return wsMilli.map(() => Math.floor(pool / n));
  return wsMilli.map((w) => Math.floor((pool * Math.floor((Math.max(0, w) * 1000) / sum)) / 1000));
}
export function sharePm(part: number, all: readonly number[]): number {
  let sum = 0;
  for (const w of all) sum += Math.max(0, w);
  return sum === 0 ? Math.floor(1000 / Math.max(1, all.length)) : Math.floor((Math.max(0, part) * 1000) / sum);
}

/** Spec §6.2: war score descending (ties: smaller smallID); rounds 2 and 4 reversed (snake). */
export function turnOrder(wsMilli: readonly number[], ids: readonly number[], round: number): number[] {
  const idx = ids.map((_, i) => i);
  idx.sort((a, b) => wsMilli[b] - wsMilli[a] || ids[a] - ids[b]);
  const out = idx.map((i) => ids[i]);
  return round % 2 === 0 ? out.reverse() : out;
}

// ---- Occupation (spec §8) ----

export const OCC_LAW = {
  occ_civil: { resPct: 70, compPct: 150, garrisonPct: 60, outputPp: -10, manpowerPp: 5 },
  occ_military: { resPct: 100, compPct: 100, garrisonPct: 100, outputPp: 0, manpowerPp: 0 },
  occ_martial: { resPct: 140, compPct: 30, garrisonPct: 150, outputPp: 15, manpowerPp: -5 },
} as const satisfies Record<OccupationLaw, object>;

export function coveragePct(supDeci: number, needDeci: number): number {
  if (needDeci <= 0) return 100;
  return Math.min(100, Math.floor((supDeci * 100) / needDeci));
}

export interface ResTargetIn {
  /** A core holder is active or an exile (spec §8.3 base 3,500). */
  coreHolderFighting: boolean;
  law: OccupationLaw;
  compBp: number;
  coveragePct: number;
  /** The best exile legitimacy among the core holders, bp. */
  exileLegitBp: number;
  /** Percentage points added (controller's resistanceTargetPct + core holders' support spirits). */
  statPct: number;
}
export function resistanceTarget(i: ResTargetIn): number {
  let t = i.coreHolderFighting ? WW_OCC_RES_BASE_FIGHTING_BP : WW_OCC_RES_BASE_BP;
  t = Math.floor((t * OCC_LAW[i.law].resPct) / 100);
  t = Math.floor((t * (10000 - Math.floor(i.compBp / 2))) / 10000);
  t = Math.floor((t * (200 - i.coveragePct)) / 100);
  t += Math.min(1000, Math.floor(i.exileLegitBp / 10));
  t += i.statPct * 100;
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(10000, t));
}
/** Daily change (spec §8.3): up at most 15 bp (upMax), down at most 30 bp × coverage. */
export function resistanceStep(resBp: number, targetBp: number, coverage: number, upMaxBp = WW_OCC_RES_UP_MAX_BP): number {
  if (targetBp > resBp) return Math.min(upMaxBp, Math.max(1, Math.ceil((targetBp - resBp) / 30)));
  if (targetBp < resBp) {
    const down = Math.min(WW_OCC_RES_DOWN_MAX_BP, Math.max(1, Math.ceil((resBp - targetBp) / 30)));
    return -Math.floor((down * coverage) / 100);
  }
  return 0;
}
export interface CompStepIn {
  compBp: number;
  resBp: number;
  law: OccupationLaw;
  statPct: number;
  capBp: number;
}
/** Daily change (spec §8.4); the caller clamps to 0..capBp. */
export function complianceStep(i: CompStepIn): number {
  const g = Math.floor(
    (WW_OCC_COMP_BASE_BP * OCC_LAW[i.law].compPct * (10000 - i.resBp) * (100 + i.statPct)) / (100 * 10000 * 100),
  );
  const d = g - (i.resBp >= 5000 ? 5 : 0);
  return Math.max(-i.compBp, Math.min(i.capBp - i.compBp, d));
}
export function complianceCap(coreHolderFighting: boolean): number {
  return coreHolderFighting ? WW_OCC_COMP_CAP_FIGHTING_BP : 10000;
}
/** Spec §8.6: (가) occupied = control ≠ legal owner; (나) annexed = someone else's core taken. */
export function occupationShare(
  kind: "occupied" | "annexed", compBp: number, law: OccupationLaw, statPp = 0,
): { factoryPct: number; manpowerPct: number } {
  const L = OCC_LAW[law];
  if (kind === "occupied") {
    return {
      factoryPct: Math.max(10, Math.min(90, 20 + Math.floor((compBp * 60) / 10000) + L.outputPp + statPp)),
      manpowerPct: Math.max(0, Math.floor((compBp * 5) / 10000) + L.manpowerPp),
    };
  }
  return {
    factoryPct: Math.max(30, Math.min(100, 50 + Math.floor((compBp * 50) / 10000) + L.outputPp + statPp)),
    manpowerPct: Math.max(0, Math.floor((compBp * 10) / 10000) + L.manpowerPp),
  };
}
/** Spec §8.5, in tenths: ⌈(10 + tiles/400) × (1 + res×2/10000) × law × (100 + stat)/100⌉. */
export function garrisonNeed(tiles: number, resBp: number, law: OccupationLaw, statPct: number): number {
  const num = (4000 + tiles) * (10000 + resBp * 2) * OCC_LAW[law].garrisonPct * (100 + statPct);
  return Math.ceil(num / (400 * 10000 * 100 * 100));
}
/** Spec §8.5, tenths per battalion / support company. */
export const BATTALION_SUPPRESS: Partial<Record<BattalionId, number>> = {
  cavalry: 20, infantry: 10, mountain: 10, motorized: 10, marine: 5, paratrooper: 5, light_armor: 5,
};
export const SUPPORT_SUPPRESS: Partial<Record<SupportId, number>> = { military_police: 30 };
export function divisionSuppression(t: TemplateDef, strengthPm: number): number {
  let s = 0;
  for (const b of t.battalions) s += BATTALION_SUPPRESS[b] ?? 0;
  for (const c of t.support) s += SUPPORT_SUPPRESS[c] ?? 0;
  return Math.floor((s * Math.max(0, Math.min(1000, strengthPm))) / 1000);
}
/** Spec §8.6: one milli of damage per day on the most numerous building. */
export function resistanceDamageMilli(resBp: number, coverage: number): number {
  if (resBp < 2500) return 0;
  return Math.floor(((resBp - 2500) * (100 - coverage)) / 100 / 50);
}
export function garrisonLossMen(men: number, resBp: number): number {
  if (resBp < 2500) return 0;
  return Math.floor((men * (resBp - 2500)) / 10000 / 400);
}
export function occLawBlock(lockUntilDay: number, day: number, ppMilli: number): WwOrderBlock | null {
  if (day < lockUntilDay) return "law_locked";
  if (ppMilli < WW_OCC_LAW_COST_PP * 1000) return "no_pp";
  return null;
}
export interface IntegrateIn {
  legalOwner: boolean;
  controls: boolean;
  hasCore: boolean;
  compBp: number;
  compHighDays: number;
  enemyHoldsCore: boolean;
  ppMilli: number;
  vp: number;
}
export function integrateCostPp(vp: number): number {
  return 25 + vp;
}
export function integrateBlock(i: IntegrateIn): WwOrderBlock | null {
  if (!i.legalOwner || !i.controls || i.hasCore || i.enemyHoldsCore) return "not_integrable";
  if (i.compBp < WW_INTEGRATE_COMP_BP || i.compHighDays < WW_INTEGRATE_DAYS) return "not_integrable";
  if (i.ppMilli < integrateCostPp(i.vp) * 1000) return "no_pp";
  return null;
}

// ---- Subjects (spec §9) ----

export function subjectShares(level: SubjectLevel): { resourcePct: number; civPct: number; manpowerPct: number } {
  switch (level) {
    case "integrated": return { resourcePct: 50, civPct: 30, manpowerPct: 30 };
    case "puppet": return { resourcePct: 30, civPct: 15, manpowerPct: 15 };
    case "colony": return { resourcePct: 15, civPct: 5, manpowerPct: 5 };
    case "dominion": return { resourcePct: 0, civPct: 0, manpowerPct: 0 };
  }
}
export interface AutonomyIn {
  subjFactories: number;
  overlordFactories: number;
  ideologyDiffers: boolean;
  fightingDivs: number;
  overlordDivsInSubject: number;
  /** Production cost of equipment received from the overlord today. */
  llFromOverlord: number;
  tightening: boolean;
  statPct: number;
}
export function autonomyDelta(i: AutonomyIn): number {
  let d = Math.min(20, Math.floor((20 * i.subjFactories) / Math.max(1, i.overlordFactories)));
  if (i.ideologyDiffers) d += 2;
  d += Math.min(5, Math.floor(i.fightingDivs / 5));
  if (i.overlordDivsInSubject >= 5) d -= 2;
  d -= Math.floor(i.llFromOverlord / 100);
  if (i.tightening) d -= 10;
  return Math.trunc((d * (100 + i.statPct)) / 100);
}

// ---- Aid (spec §12, §13) ----

export function volunteerCap(senderDivs: number, targetDivs: number): number {
  return Math.min(Math.ceil((senderDivs * 10) / 100), Math.max(2, Math.floor(targetDivs / 3)), WW_VOLUNTEER_MAX_DIV);
}
export interface VolunteerIn {
  self: boolean;
  targetAtWar: boolean;
  atWarWithTargetEnemy: boolean;
  atWarWithTarget: boolean;
  senderDemocracy: boolean;
  tensionPct: number;
  senderDivs: number;
  targetDivs: number;
  alreadySent: number;
  armyDivs: number;
  /** A spirit rule forbids aid to this target (spec §19.1 non-intervention). */
  ruleBlocks: boolean;
}
export function volunteerBlock(i: VolunteerIn): WwOrderBlock | null {
  if (i.self || !i.targetAtWar || i.atWarWithTargetEnemy || i.atWarWithTarget || i.ruleBlocks) return "volunteer_conditions";
  if (i.senderDemocracy && i.tensionPct < WW_VOLUNTEER_TENSION_PCT) return "volunteer_conditions";
  if (i.senderDivs < WW_VOLUNTEER_MIN_DIV) return "volunteer_conditions";
  if (i.alreadySent + i.armyDivs > volunteerCap(i.senderDivs, i.targetDivs)) return "volunteer_limit";
  return null;
}
export interface LlIn {
  self: boolean;
  atWarWithEachOther: boolean;
  bothActors: boolean;
  receiverAtWar: boolean;
  tensionPct: number;
  senderDemocracy: boolean;
  senderLendLeaseFlag: boolean;
  senderNoLendLeaseRule: boolean;
}
/** Spec §13.2: null when a contract may run today. */
export function llBlock(i: LlIn): WwOrderBlock | null {
  if (i.self || i.atWarWithEachOther || !i.bothActors) return "ll_conditions";
  if (!i.receiverAtWar && i.tensionPct < WW_LL_TENSION_RECEIVER_PCT) return "ll_conditions";
  if (i.senderNoLendLeaseRule) return "ll_conditions";
  if (i.senderDemocracy && !i.senderLendLeaseFlag && i.tensionPct < WW_LL_TENSION_DEMOCRACY_PCT) return "ll_conditions";
  return null;
}
export function llLimitBlock(contracts: number): WwOrderBlock | null {
  return contracts >= WW_LL_MAX_CONTRACTS ? "ll_limit" : null;
}
/** Spec §13.3: industry 10 = one convoy over 5,000 km. */
export function lendLeaseConvoys(dailyCost: number, routeKm: number): number {
  return Math.ceil((dailyCost * routeKm) / 50000);
}
export function lendLeaseSeaDays(routeKm: number, kmPerDay: number): number {
  return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(routeKm / kmPerDay));
}
/** Spec §14: captured milli of `lostMilli`. */
export function captureMilli(lostMilli: number, captureBp: number): number {
  return Math.floor((lostMilli * captureBp) / 10000);
}

// ---- Civil war (spec §15) ----

export function civilWarShareBp(popBp: number): number {
  return Math.max(WW_CIVIL_SHARE_MIN_BP, Math.min(WW_CIVIL_SHARE_MAX_BP, popBp));
}
export interface RegionGraphView {
  /** Land neighbours, ascending. */
  neighbours(region: number): readonly number[];
}
/**
 * Spec §15.2: the rebel regions. BFS from the seeds (content seeds, else the
 * candidate farthest from the capital, ties: smaller id) over candidates in
 * id order until the rebels' points reach ⌈total × share⌉; then unreached
 * candidates by points (desc, ties id) until the share is met; finally each
 * 0-point candidate joins the side with more neighbours (ties: the government).
 * The capital never joins. Sorted ascending.
 */
export function splitRegionsForCivilWar(
  graph: RegionGraphView, candidates: readonly number[], capital: number, seeds: readonly number[],
  vp: (r: number) => number, shareBp: number,
): number[] {
  const cand = [...new Set(candidates)].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const isCand = new Set(cand);
  let total = 0;
  for (const r of cand) total += vp(r);
  const want = Math.ceil((total * shareBp) / 10000);
  const dist = new Map<number, number>();
  if (isCand.has(capital)) {
    dist.set(capital, 0);
    const q = [capital];
    for (let h = 0; h < q.length; h++) {
      for (const n of graph.neighbours(q[h])) {
        if (!isCand.has(n) || dist.has(n)) continue;
        dist.set(n, dist.get(q[h])! + 1);
        q.push(n);
      }
    }
  }
  let start = seeds.filter((r) => isCand.has(r) && r !== capital).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  if (start.length === 0) {
    let best = 0;
    let bestD = 0;
    for (const r of cand) {
      const d = dist.get(r);
      if (r === capital || d === undefined) continue;
      if (d > bestD) {
        best = r;
        bestD = d;
      }
    }
    if (best !== 0) start = [best];
  }
  const rebel = new Set<number>();
  const seen = new Set<number>(start);
  let got = 0;
  const q = [...start];
  for (let h = 0; h < q.length && got < want; h++) {
    const r = q[h];
    rebel.add(r);
    got += vp(r);
    for (const n of graph.neighbours(r)) {
      if (!isCand.has(n) || n === capital || seen.has(n)) continue;
      seen.add(n);
      q.push(n);
    }
  }
  if (got < want) {
    const rest = cand.filter((r) => r !== capital && !seen.has(r)).sort((a, b) => vp(b) - vp(a) || a - b);
    for (const r of rest) {
      if (got >= want) break;
      rebel.add(r);
      got += vp(r);
    }
  }
  for (const r of cand) {
    if (r === capital || vp(r) !== 0) continue;
    let reb = 0;
    let gov = 0;
    for (const n of graph.neighbours(r)) {
      if (!isCand.has(n) || n === r) continue;
      if (rebel.has(n)) reb++;
      else gov++;
    }
    if (reb > gov) rebel.add(r);
    else rebel.delete(r);
  }
  return [...rebel].sort((a, b) => a - b);
}

/** Spec §3.5: lightness ±18%, hue 20° toward the ideology's hue. Integer HSL (h°, s/l ‰). */
export const IDEOLOGY_HUE: Readonly<Record<Ideology, number>> = { democratic: 215, communist: 0, fascist: 30, neutral: 270 };
export function civilWarColor(parent: readonly [number, number, number], ideology: Ideology): [number, number, number] {
  const [h, s, l] = rgbToHsl(parent);
  const diff = ((IDEOLOGY_HUE[ideology] - h + 540) % 360) - 180;
  const step = diff === 0 ? 0 : Math.sign(diff) * Math.min(20, Math.abs(diff));
  return hslToRgb((h + step + 360) % 360, s, l < 500 ? Math.min(1000, l + 180) : Math.max(0, l - 180));
}
export function rgbToHsl([r, g, b]: readonly [number, number, number]): [number, number, number] {
  const max = Math.max(r, g, b);
  const min = Math.min(r, g, b);
  const l = Math.floor(((max + min) * 1000) / 510);
  if (max === min) return [0, 0, l];
  const d = max - min;
  const s = max + min > 255 ? Math.floor((d * 1000) / (510 - max - min)) : Math.floor((d * 1000) / (max + min));
  let h: number;
  if (max === r) h = Math.floor((60 * (g - b)) / d);
  else if (max === g) h = Math.floor((60 * (b - r)) / d) + 120;
  else h = Math.floor((60 * (r - g)) / d) + 240;
  return [((h % 360) + 360) % 360, Math.min(1000, s), l];
}
export function hslToRgb(h: number, s: number, l: number): [number, number, number] {
  const c = Math.floor(((1000 - Math.abs(2 * l - 1000)) * s) / 1000);
  const x = Math.floor((c * (1000 - Math.abs(Math.floor(((h % 120) * 1000) / 60) - 1000))) / 1000);
  const m = l - Math.floor(c / 2);
  const sectors: [number, number, number][] = [[c, x, 0], [x, c, 0], [0, c, x], [0, x, c], [x, 0, c], [c, 0, x]];
  const [r1, g1, b1] = sectors[Math.floor(h / 60) % 6];
  const to = (v: number) => Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.floor(((v + m) * 255 + 500) / 1000)));
  return [to(r1), to(g1), to(b1)];
}
export function packRgb([r, g, b]: readonly [number, number, number]): number {
  return (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;
}
/** Spec §3.5: ww1936.cw_name.<ideology> with {country}. */
export function civilWarNameKey(ideology: Ideology): string {
  return `ww1936.cw_name.${ideology}`;
}
export type { CountryTag };
```

테스트 `EndgameRules.test.ts`(표 테스트, 각 이름이 한 `it`):
- "base value follows spec §6.3": `regionBaseValue(35, 6, 3200) === 87`(바르샤바 예), `demandCost(87, {controls: true, …false}) === 43`, 통제 + 코어 = 21, 모든 거짓 = 87, `demandCost(1, {controls: true, core: true, …}) === 1`(최소 1).
- "points never exceed the pool and follow shares": `participantPoints(100, [700, 290, 10])` = `[91, 37, 1]`(P 130, ‰ 700/290/10), 합 ≤ 130; `[0, 0]` → `[65, 65]`; `[]` → `[]`.
- "snake order": `turnOrder([5, 9, 9], [3, 2, 7], 1)` = `[2, 7, 3]`, 라운드 2 = `[3, 7, 2]`.
- "resistance target": 바탕 3,500·군정·준수도 0·충족 100·정통성 0·통계 0 → 3,500; 충족 0 → 7,000; 계엄 → 4,900; 준수도 10,000 → 1,750; 정통성 5,000 → +500; 결과는 0..10,000.
- "resistance step": (0, 6000, 0) = +15; (5900, 6000, 0) = +4; (6000, 0, 0) = 0(수비대 없으면 안 내려감); (6000, 0, 100) = −30; (6000, 0, 50) = −15; (100, 99, 100) = −1·... (`ceil(1/30)=1`).
- "compliance step and cap": 군정·저항 0 → +8, 민정 → +12, 계엄 → +2, 저항 5,000 → `floor(8 × 0.5) − 5 = −1`, 상한 넘지 않음, 0 아래 없음.
- "shares at the edges": `occupationShare("occupied", 0, "occ_military")` = 20/0, 10,000 → 80/5, 계엄 10,000 → 90(상한)/0, 민정 0 → 10/5; `annexed` 0 → 50/0, 10,000 → 100/10.
- "garrison need": `garrisonNeed(3000, 4000, "occ_military", 0) === 32`(spec §8.5 예), 민정 → 19, 통계 −15 → 27.
- "suppression": 보병 9 사단 전력 1000 → 90, 기병 4 + 헌병 → 110, 전력 500 → 절반 내림.
- "autonomy": 캐나다 예(18/90) → +4, 이념 다름 +2, 싸운 사단 12 → +2, 주둔 5 → −2, 랜드리스 250 → −2, 통제 강화 −10, 통계 +50% → `trunc`.
- "volunteer cap and block": `volunteerCap(50, 30) === 5`, `(200, 60) === 12`(상한), `(12, 3) === 2`; 민주·긴장 20 → `volunteer_conditions`; 상한 넘음 → `volunteer_limit`.
- "lend-lease conditions": spec §13.2 표의 여섯 줄, `lendLeaseConvoys(40, 7500) === 6`(spec §13.3 예).
- "civil war share is clamped": 2,000 → 3,000, 8,000 → 7,000.
- "civil war colours are integer and stable": `civilWarColor([200, 60, 70], "communist")` 두 번 같은 값, 성분 0..255 정수; `hslToRgb(...rgbToHsl(c))`가 c와 성분마다 ±2 안(무작위 색 1,000개).

`CivilWarSplit.test.ts`(spec §23.1): 줄 그래프 1–2–3–4–5(모두 승점 10, 수도 1) 몫 3,000 → `[4, 5]`(씨앗 = 가장 먼 5, BFS 5→4), 5,000 → `[3, 4, 5]`, 7,000 → `[2, 3, 4, 5]`(원하는 35에 40으로 닿음); 씨앗 `[2]`면 `[2, 3]`(3,000: 20 ≥ 15); 끊긴 주 6(승점 30, 간선 없음)을 더한 그래프에서 7,000 → BFS로 2~5(40)를 넣어도 56에 못 미치므로 6이 들어가 `[2, 3, 4, 5, 6]`; 승점 0 주 7(간선 3–7, 4–7, 5–7)은 3,000에서 반군(이웃 4·5 반군 2 > 정부 1), 간선을 3–7, 4–7만 두면 정부(1 대 1); 같은 입력 두 번 같은 결과.

- [ ] **Step 6: 콘텐츠 형식과 등록** (결정 29, 30)

`content/types.ts`에 `PeaceDefaultDef`, `PeaceDefaultLine`, `PeaceDefaultDemand`, `CoreDef`, `CivilWarSeedDef`, `OccupationDefaultDef`를 재수출. 빈 자료 파일(이름 = export, 모양):

```text
content/focus/ita.ts        ITA_FOCUSES: FocusDef[], ITA_PATH: FocusId[], ITA_BRANCHES: BranchPoint[]   (jpn/fra/usa 같은 모양)
content/events/{ita,jpn,fra,usa,endgame}.ts      <X>_EVENTS: EventDef[]          (ENDGAME_EVENTS)
content/decisions/{ita,jpn,fra,usa,endgame}.ts   <X>_DECISIONS: DecisionDef[]    (ENDGAME_DECISIONS)
content/spirits/{ita,jpn,fra,usa,endgame}.ts     SPIRITS_<X>: SpiritDef[]        (SPIRITS_ENDGAME)
content/persons/{ita,jpn,fra,usa}.ts             PERSONS_<X>: PersonDef[]
content/templates/{ita,jpn,fra,usa}.ts           TEMPLATES_<X>: TemplateUnlockDef[]
content/flags/{ita,jpn,fra,usa,endgame}.ts       FLAGS_<X>: string[]
content/endgame/peace.ts       HISTORY_PEACE: PeaceDefaultDef[]
content/endgame/cores.ts       CORES_1936: CoreDef[]
content/endgame/groups.ts      REGION_GROUPS_P5: RegionGroupDef[]
content/endgame/beats.ts       BEATS_P5: HistoryBeat[]
content/endgame/dormant.ts     DORMANT_POLITICS: CountryPoliticsDef[]
content/endgame/claims.ts      CLAIMS_P5: ClaimDef[]
content/endgame/seeds.ts       CIVIL_WAR_SEEDS: CivilWarSeedDef[]
content/endgame/occupation.ts  OCCUPATION_DEFAULTS: OccupationDefaultDef[]
```

`ContentParts`에 `peace`, `cores`, `seeds`, `occupationDefaults` 네 필드를 더하고 `CONTENT_PARTS`를 이어 붙인다(파일 순서 = 이벤트 번호 순서이므로 **P3 묶음 뒤에** P5 묶음):

```ts
  focuses: [...P3 focuses..., ...ITA_FOCUSES, ...JPN_FOCUSES, ...FRA_FOCUSES, ...USA_FOCUSES],
  paths: { ...P3, ITA: ITA_PATH, JPN: JPN_PATH, FRA: FRA_PATH, USA: USA_PATH },
  branchPoints: { ...P3, ITA: ITA_BRANCHES, JPN: JPN_BRANCHES, FRA: FRA_BRANCHES, USA: USA_BRANCHES },
  events: [...P3 events..., ...ITA_EVENTS, ...JPN_EVENTS, ...FRA_EVENTS, ...USA_EVENTS, ...ENDGAME_EVENTS],
  decisions: [...P3..., ...ITA_DECISIONS, ...JPN_DECISIONS, ...FRA_DECISIONS, ...USA_DECISIONS, ...ENDGAME_DECISIONS],
  spirits: [...P3..., ...SPIRITS_ITA, ...SPIRITS_JPN, ...SPIRITS_FRA, ...SPIRITS_USA, ...SPIRITS_ENDGAME],
  persons: [...P3..., ...PERSONS_ITA, ...PERSONS_JPN, ...PERSONS_FRA, ...PERSONS_USA],
  templates: [...P3..., ...TEMPLATES_ITA, ...TEMPLATES_JPN, ...TEMPLATES_FRA, ...TEMPLATES_USA],
  flags: [...P3..., ...FLAGS_ITA, ...FLAGS_JPN, ...FLAGS_FRA, ...FLAGS_USA, ...FLAGS_ENDGAME, ...FLAGS_P5_BASE],
  beats: [...HISTORY_BEATS, ...BEATS_P5],
  groups: [...REGION_GROUPS, ...REGION_GROUPS_P5],
  countries: [...COUNTRY_POLITICS, ...DORMANT_POLITICS],
  claims: mergeClaims(CLAIMS, CLAIMS_P5),   // same tag: groups concatenated, first order kept
  peace: HISTORY_PEACE, cores: CORES_1936, seeds: CIVIL_WAR_SEEDS, occupationDefaults: OCCUPATION_DEFAULTS,
```

`mergeClaims`는 `registry.ts` 안의 함수(콘텐츠 파일이 아니다). `ContentRegistry`에 파생 `peaceByLoser: Map<CountryTag, PeaceDefaultDef[]>`, `coresByTag: Map<CountryTag, string[]>`. `flags/base.ts` 옆에 `FLAGS_P5_BASE`(기반, `content/flags/endgame.ts`가 아니라 `registry.ts`가 아는 상수 — spec이 이름을 정한 깃발): `lend_lease`(P3에 이미 있으면 빼고), `independence_denied`, `control_tightened`, `autonomy_full`, `unconditional_surrender`, `second_front_agreed`, `yalta_zones`, `potsdam_lines`, `pledged_vs_japan`, `ita_july_25`, `talks_continue`, `heavy_water`, `radar_lab`. 갈래가 새로 쓰는 깃발은 자기 `flags/<갈래>.ts`.

앞 단계 콘텐츠의 한 줄 고침(결정 30): P3 `countries.ts`의 ITA·JPN·FRA·USA `tree`를 `"ITA"`·`"JPN"`·`"FRA"`·`"USA"`로; `usa_isolation`(spirits/start.ts)·`usa_isolation_cracked`(spirits/world.ts)의 `rules`에 `"no_lend_lease"`; P0 안전망 비트 `1936-07-17 ESN → ESR`(history.ts)에 `civil: true`; P3 `ENGINE_EVENTS`에 `gen_subject_upgrade`, `gen_diplo_lend_lease`, `gen_exile_formed`, `gen_uprising`, `gen_peace_conference`, `gen_volunteers_arrive`. 트리를 바꾼 네 나라는 P3 `GENERIC_PATH`를 쓰지 않는다(spec §18.1-5) — P3 중점 AI가 `paths[tree]`를 읽으므로 자료가 비어 있는 동안(K1~K4 합치기 전) 그 네 나라는 중점을 고르지 않는다(정상; Phase B 뒤 돈다).

- [ ] **Step 7: 끝 계통 상태 `packages/engine/src/ww1936/endgame/WwEndgame.ts`** (spec §21.1, 결정 2~9, 14, 17~20)

```ts
import { MessageType, PlayerType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";

import type { WwOrder } from "@openfront/engine-api/Schemas";

  CS_ACTIVE, CS_DORMANT, CS_GONE, COUNTRY_STATES, CountryState, DEMAND_KINDS, MEMBER_STATES, MemberState,
  OCCUPATION_LAWS, OccupationLaw, SUBJECT_LEVELS, SubjectLevel, WAR_KINDS, WarLink, WW_CORE_SLOTS,
  WW_PEACE_LOG_SIZE, WW_WAR_KEEP_DAYS, WwEndgameData, WwPeaceLogEntry, WwWar,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/EndgameTypes";
```
