```ts
import { garrisonOrder, integrateRegion, regionShareOf, setOccupationLaw, stepOccupation, supplyFactorOf } from "./Occupation";
import { assignGarrisons } from "./Garrison";
import { onOverlordWar, startSubjects, stepSubject, subjectBlock, subjectEconomy, tradeCivFactorPct } from "./Subjects";
import { exileEconomy, exilePpFactorPct, resolveTakeovers, stepExile, takeoverPick } from "./Exile";
import { returnLiberated } from "./Liberation";
import { aidEventOption, deliverLendLease, issueAid, stepLendLease, volunteerCanEnter } from "./LendLease";
import { checkWarEnds, dailyWarScore, reparationsEconomy, disarmBlockOf } from "./Wars";
import { aiOccupationDay } from "./AiOccupation";
import { aiSubjectsDay } from "./AiSubjects";
import { aiAidDay } from "./AiAid";
import { wakePending } from "./Executions";

const zIds = () => z.array(zInt());
const StoredDemandSchema = z.object({
  kind: zInt(), slot: zInt(), regions: zIds(), target: zInt(), beneficiary: zInt(), k: zInt(), pct: zInt(),
});
export type StoredDemand = z.infer<typeof StoredDemandSchema>;
const SettledSchema = z.object({
  kind: zInt(), by: zInt(), regions: zIds(), target: zInt(), beneficiary: zInt(), k: zInt(), pct: zInt(),
  round: zInt(), cost: zInt(),
});
export type Settled = z.infer<typeof SettledSchema>;
const ConferenceSchema = z.object({
  id: zInt(), warIds: zIds(), losers: zIds(), stake: zIds(), base: zIds(),
  parts: z.array(z.object({ id: zInt(), wsMilli: zInt(), pts: zInt(), human: z.boolean() })),
  claims: z.array(z.tuple([zInt(), zInt()])), conquer: z.array(z.tuple([zInt(), zInt()])),
  topple: z.array(z.tuple([zInt(), zInt()])),
  /** 0 queued, 1 open, 2 done. */
  status: zInt(), round: zInt(), roundEndTick: zInt(), openTick: zInt(),
  subs: z.array(z.object({ id: zInt(), demands: z.array(StoredDemandSchema), passed: z.boolean() })),
  ai: z.array(z.object({ id: zInt(), demands: z.array(StoredDemandSchema) })),
  settled: z.array(SettledSchema),
  lastRound: z.array(z.object({ by: zInt(), kind: zInt(), regions: zIds(), target: zInt(), ok: z.boolean() })),
});
export type Conference = z.infer<typeof ConferenceSchema>;
const WarSchema = z.object({
  id: zInt(), kind: z.enum(WAR_KINDS), startDay: zInt(),
  sides: z.array(z.object({
    leader: zInt(),
    members: z.array(z.object({ id: zInt(), state: z.enum(MEMBER_STATES), joinedDay: zInt(), wsMilli: zInt() })),
  })).length(2),
  goal: z.object({ type: z.string(), group: z.string() }).nullable(),
  ended: z.object({ day: zInt(), winner: zInt() }).nullable(),
});
const SubjectSchema = z.object({
  subject: zInt(), overlord: zInt(), level: zInt(), autBp: zInt(), neutral: z.boolean(),
  deniedDay: zInt(), tightenUntil: zInt(), lastDelta: zInt(), suspended: z.boolean(),
  flowRes: zIds(), flowCiv: zInt(), flowMp: zInt(),
  /** Production cost of equipment received from the overlord today (A adds, S reads and clears). */
  llToday: zInt(),
});
export type Subject = z.infer<typeof SubjectSchema>;
const ExileSchema = z.object({
  smallID: zInt(), host: zInt(), legitBp: zInt(), sinceDay: zInt(), zeroDays: zInt(), lastDelta: zInt(),
  /** P3 faction index at exile time (takeover candidates, spec §11.2); -1: none. */
  faction: zInt(), capital: zInt(),
});
export type Exile = z.infer<typeof ExileSchema>;
const LendLeaseSchema = z.object({
  id: zInt(), from: zInt(), to: zInt(), item: z.string(), gen: zInt(), perMonth: zInt(), sentMilli: zInt(),
  startDay: zInt(), blocked: z.string().nullable(), monthStart: zInt(), sentMonth: zInt(), arrivedMonth: zInt(),
  route: zInt(),
});
export type LendLease = z.infer<typeof LendLeaseSchema>;
const InTransitSchema = z.object({
  contract: zInt(), from: zInt(), to: zInt(), item: z.string(), gen: zInt(), countMilli: zInt(),
  arriveDay: zInt(), srs: zIds(), sea: z.boolean(), costMilli: zInt(),
});
export type InTransit = z.infer<typeof InTransitSchema>;
export const WwEndgameStateSchema = z.object({
  gameID: z.string().max(64),
  lastCommonDay: zInt(),
  countryState: zIds(),
  nameKey: z.array(z.string().nullable()),
  nameArg: z.array(z.string().nullable()),
  color: zIds(),                                   // -1: none
  controllerOf: z.array(z.tuple([z.string(), zInt()])),
  everHuman: z.array(z.tuple([z.string(), zInt()])), // clientID → first country (MVP "now (first)")
  wars: z.array(WarSchema), nextWarId: zInt(),
  pairWar: z.array(z.tuple([zInt(), zInt(), zInt()])),
  conferences: z.array(ConferenceSchema), nextConfId: zInt(),
  peaceLog: z.array(z.object({
    day: zInt(), losers: zIds(),
    lines: z.array(z.object({ key: z.string(), params: z.record(z.string(), z.union([z.string(), zInt()])) })),
  })),
  /** [region, holder1, holder2, holder3] for every region with a holder. */
  cores: z.array(z.tuple([zInt(), zInt(), zInt(), zInt()])),
  /** [region, resBp, compBp, sinceDay, compHighDays, uprisingDays, supDeci] where any is non-zero. */
  occ: z.array(z.tuple([zInt(), zInt(), zInt(), zInt(), zInt(), zInt(), zInt()])),
  occLaw: z.array(z.object({ by: zInt(), of: zInt(), law: zInt(), lockUntilDay: zInt() })),
  zones: z.array(z.object({ region: zInt(), holder: zInt(), untilDay: zInt() })),
  dmz: z.array(z.tuple([zInt(), zInt()])),
  subjects: z.array(SubjectSchema),
  exiles: z.array(ExileSchema),
  lendLease: z.array(LendLeaseSchema), inTransit: z.array(InTransitSchema), nextLlId: zInt(),
  volunteers: z.array(z.object({ army: zInt(), from: zInt(), to: zInt(), sinceDay: zInt(), recall: z.boolean() })),
  reparations: z.array(z.object({ from: zInt(), to: zInt(), k: zInt(), untilDay: zInt() })),
  disarm: z.array(z.tuple([zInt(), zInt()])),
  takeoverCards: z.array(z.object({
    client: z.string(), from: zInt(), candidates: zIds(), deadlineTick: zInt(), pick: zInt(),
  })),
  wakeQueue: zIds(),
  /** Daily one-line batches (spec §20.8): [to, kind, a, b, n]. Kinds: 0 capture, 1 ll arrival, 2 garrison short. */
  dayNotes: z.array(z.tuple([zInt(), zInt(), zInt(), zInt(), zInt()])),
});
export type WwEndgameState = z.infer<typeof WwEndgameStateSchema>;

/** spec §25.3. Lanes call these fields and methods; they never keep their own copies. */
export class WwEndgame {
  readonly size: number;
  readonly regionCap: number;
  gameID = "";
  lastCommonDay = -1;
  countryStateArr: Uint8Array;
  nameKey: (string | null)[];
  nameArg: (string | null)[];
  color: number[];
  controllerOf: [string, number][] = [];
  everHuman: [string, number][] = [];
  wars: WwWar[] = [];
  nextWarId = 1;
  pairWar = new Map<number, number>();
  conferences: Conference[] = [];
  nextConfId = 1;
  peaceLog: WwPeaceLogEntry[] = [];
  coreTable: Uint16Array;
  resBp: Int16Array;
  compBp: Int16Array;
  occSince: Int32Array;
  compHigh: Uint16Array;
  uprDays: Uint8Array;
  supDeci: Int32Array;
  occLaw: { by: number; of: number; law: number; lockUntilDay: number }[] = [];
  zones: { region: number; holder: number; untilDay: number }[] = [];
  dmz: [number, number][] = [];
  subjects: Subject[] = [];
  exiles: Exile[] = [];
  lendLease: LendLease[] = [];
  inTransit: InTransit[] = [];
  nextLlId = 1;
  volunteers: { army: number; from: number; to: number; sinceDay: number; recall: boolean }[] = [];
  reparations: { from: number; to: number; k: number; untilDay: number }[] = [];
  disarm: [number, number][] = [];
  takeoverCards: { client: string; from: number; candidates: number[]; deadlineTick: number; pick: number }[] = [];
  wakeQueue: number[] = [];
  dayNotes: [number, number, number, number, number][] = [];

  // ---- Derived (DERIVED_FIELDS) ----
  /** smallID → clientID of its human controller (plan decision 7). */
  humanOf = new Map<number, string>();
  /** controller smallID → administered regions, ascending (plan decision 18). */
  adminByController = new Map<number, number[]>();
  adminSet = new Set<number>();
  adminDirty = true;
  subjectIdx = new Map<number, number>();
  exileIdx = new Map<number, number>();
  warsOfIdx = new Map<number, number[]>();
  endgameNews: { id: string; params: Record<string, string | number> }[] = [];
  dirtyCountries = new Set<number>();
  dirtyWars = false;
  dirtyConf = false;
  dirtyPeaceLog = false;
  dirtyCores = new Set<number>();
  dirtyOcc = new Set<number>();
  dirtySubjects = false;
  dirtyExiles = false;
  dirtyZones = false;
  dirtyAid = new Set<number>();
  dirtyControllers = false;
  dirtyTakeover = false;
  firstData = true;

  constructor(
    readonly mg: Game,
    readonly ww: WorldWar,
  ) {
    this.size = ww.scenario.countries.length + 1;
    let maxR = 0;
    for (const r of ww.scenario.landRegions) maxR = Math.max(maxR, r.id);
    this.regionCap = maxR + 1;
    this.countryStateArr = new Uint8Array(this.size);
    this.nameKey = new Array<string | null>(this.size).fill(null);
    this.nameArg = new Array<string | null>(this.size).fill(null);
    this.color = new Array<number>(this.size).fill(-1);
    this.coreTable = new Uint16Array(this.regionCap * WW_CORE_SLOTS);
    this.resBp = new Int16Array(this.regionCap);
    this.compBp = new Int16Array(this.regionCap);
    this.occSince = new Int32Array(this.regionCap);
    this.compHigh = new Uint16Array(this.regionCap);
    this.uprDays = new Uint8Array(this.regionCap);
    this.supDeci = new Int32Array(this.regionCap);
  }

  /** Spec §3.1, §7.1, §9.4: country states, 1936 cores, starting subjects, starting war records. */
  start(): void {
    const sc = this.ww.scenario;
    for (let id = 1; id < this.size; id++) {
      this.countryStateArr[id] = isDormantTag(sc.countries[id - 1]) ? CS_DORMANT : CS_ACTIVE;
    }
    for (const r of sc.landRegions) if (r.core) this.addCore(sc.ownerByRegion()[r.id], r.id);
    const content = this.ww.politics.content;
    for (const c of content.cores) {
      const id = sc.smallIdOf(c.tag);
      if (id === 0) continue;
      for (const g of c.groups) for (const r of content.groupIdx.get(g)?.regions ?? []) this.addCore(id, r);
    }
    startSubjects(this); // Subjects.ts (S): spec §9.4 table; foundation stub = the table, levels and autonomy only
    for (const p of this.mg.allPlayers()) {
      if (p.type() === PlayerType.Human && p.clientID() !== null) this.everHuman.push([p.clientID()!, p.smallID()]);
    }
    this.everHuman.sort((a, b) => (a[0] < b[0] ? -1 : a[0] > b[0] ? 1 : 0));
    this.rebuildDerived();
  }

  setGameID(id: string): void {
    this.gameID = id;
  }

  // ---- Clock (spec §2.2; WorldWarExecution calls clock after politics.tick, tick after air.tick) ----

  clock(ticks: number): void {
    if (this.ww.inPrep(ticks)) return;
    const day = this.ww.day(ticks);
    if (day !== this.lastCommonDay) {
      this.lastCommonDay = day;
      this.dailyCommon(day, ticks);
    }
    const k = ticks - dayStartTick(day, this.ww.prepEndTick());
    if (k < 0 || k >= WW_DAY_SPLIT) return;
    for (let id = 1; id < this.size; id++) if (id % WW_DAY_SPLIT === k) this.dailyCountry(id, day, ticks);
  }

  /** Spec §2.2 order 2. */
  dailyCommon(day: number, tick: number): void {
    if (this.adminDirty) this.rebuildAdmin();
    returnLiberated(this, day, tick);            // X: spec §5.3
    this.expireZones(day);
    dailyWarScore(this, day);                    // W: occupation points (spec §4.5)
    checkWarEnds(this, day, tick);               // W: spec §4.4, opens conferences
    deliverLendLease(this, day);                 // A: spec §13.3
    this.dropOldWars(day);
    this.expireTimed(day);
  }

  /** Spec §2.2 order 3: smallID % 7 === k. */
  dailyCountry(id: number, day: number, tick: number): void {
    if (!this.isActor(id)) return;
    if (this.adminDirty) this.rebuildAdmin();
    stepOccupation(this, id, day);               // O
    assignGarrisons(this, id, day);              // O
    if (this.subjectIdx.has(id)) stepSubject(this, id, day);   // S
    if (this.exileIdx.has(id)) stepExile(this, id, day);       // X
    stepLendLease(this, id, day);                // A
    if (!this.isHumanControlled(id)) {
      aiOccupationDay(this, id, day, tick);      // O
      aiSubjectsDay(this, id, day, tick);        // S
      aiAidDay(this, id, day, tick);             // A
    }
    this.flushDayNotes(id);
  }

  /** Spec §2.2 order 8. */
  tick(tick: number): void {
    stepConferences(this, tick);                 // W: deadlines, AI-only conferences (plan decision 33)
    resolveTakeovers(this, tick);                // X
    wakePending(this, tick);                     // foundation: addCountryExecutions for wakeQueue
  }

  // ---- Orders (plan decision 10) ----

  issue(smallID: number, order: WwOrder, tick: number): WwOrderBlock | null {
    const day = this.ww.day(tick);
    switch (order.kind) {
      case "peace_submit": return submitDemands(this, smallID, order.conf, order.demands, tick);
      case "peace_pass": return submitDemands(this, smallID, order.conf, null, tick);
      case "occupation_law": return setOccupationLaw(this, smallID, order.of, order.law, day);
      case "integrate_region": return integrateRegion(this, smallID, order.region, day);
      case "lend_lease": case "lend_lease_cancel": case "lend_lease_request":
      case "volunteers_send": case "volunteers_recall": case "air_volunteers": case "ships_transfer":
        return issueAid(this, smallID, order, tick);
      case "takeover_pick": return takeoverPick(this, smallID, order.smallID, tick);
      case "army_garrison": return garrisonOrder(this, smallID, order.army, order.scope);
      default: return "bad_order";
    }
  }

  // ---- Capitulation (plan decision 9) ----

  onCapitulated(loser: number, victor: number, tick: number): void {
    capitulate(this, loser, victor, tick);       // W (stub mimics P0–P4)
    this.adminDirty = true;
  }

  // ---- War records (spec §4.2–§4.3, plan decision 8; foundation bodies) ----

  static pairKey(a: number, b: number, size: number): number {
    return Math.min(a, b) * size + Math.max(a, b);
  }
  war(id: number): WwWar | null {
    return this.wars.find((w) => w.id === id) ?? null;
  }
  warOfPair(a: number, b: number): number {
    return this.pairWar.get(WwEndgame.pairKey(a, b, this.size)) ?? 0;
  }
  warsOf(id: number): readonly number[] {
    return this.warsOfIdx.get(id) ?? [];
  }
  sideIndex(w: WwWar, id: number): 0 | 1 | -1 {
    if (w.sides[0].members.some((m) => m.id === id)) return 0;
    if (w.sides[1].members.some((m) => m.id === id)) return 1;
    return -1;
  }
  member(w: WwWar, id: number) {
    const s = this.sideIndex(w, id);
    return s < 0 ? null : w.sides[s].members.find((m) => m.id === id)!;
  }
  /** Oldest ongoing war `id` belongs to (any state but left); 0 if none. */
  oldestWarOf(id: number): number {
    for (const w of this.wars) {
      if (w.ended !== null) continue;
      const m = this.member(w, id);
      if (m !== null && m.state !== "left") return w.id;
    }
    return 0;
  }
  onWarPairCreated(a: number, b: number, link: WarLink, tick: number): number {
    const day = this.ww.inPrep(tick) ? 0 : this.ww.day(tick);
    let w: WwWar | null = null;
    if (link.kind === "into") w = this.war(link.warId === 0 ? this.oldestWarOf(b) : link.warId);
    if (w === null || w.ended !== null || this.sideIndex(w, b) < 0) {
      w = {
        id: this.nextWarId++, kind: link.kind === "declare" ? (link.war ?? "normal") : "normal", startDay: day,
        sides: [{ leader: a, members: [] }, { leader: b, members: [] }],
        goal: link.kind === "declare" && link.goal ? { type: link.goal.type, group: link.goal.group ?? "" } : null,
        ended: null,
      };
      this.wars.push(w);
      this.addMember(w, 0, a, day);
      this.addMember(w, 1, b, day);
    } else {
      const sb = this.sideIndex(w, b) as 0 | 1;
      const sa = (1 - sb) as 0 | 1;
      const existing = this.member(w, a);
      if (existing === null) this.addMember(w, sa, a, day);
      else if (existing.state === "left") existing.state = "active";
    }
    this.pairWar.set(WwEndgame.pairKey(a, b, this.size), w.id);
    this.rebuildWarIdx();
    this.dirtyWars = true;
    onOverlordWar(this, a, b, false, tick);   // S: subjects follow (spec §9.1)
    onOverlordWar(this, b, a, true, tick);
    return w.id;
  }
  private addMember(w: WwWar, side: 0 | 1, id: number, day: number): void {
    w.sides[side].members.push({ id, state: this.isExile(id) ? "exiled" : "active", joinedDay: day, wsMilli: 0 });
  }
  /** P3 end_war / P0 endWarsOf: the pair is gone; a member with no pair left in that war has left (spec §4.3). */
  onWarPairRemoved(a: number, b: number, _tick: number): void {
    const key = WwEndgame.pairKey(a, b, this.size);
    const wid = this.pairWar.get(key);
    if (wid === undefined) return;
    this.pairWar.delete(key);
    const w = this.war(wid);
    if (w !== null && w.ended === null) {
      for (const x of [a, b]) {
        const m = this.member(w, x);
        if (m === null || m.state !== "active") continue;
        let left = true;
        for (const [k, v] of this.pairWar) if (v === wid && (Math.floor(k / this.size) === x || k % this.size === x)) left = false;
        if (left) m.state = "left";
      }
      this.fixLeader(w);
    }
    this.rebuildWarIdx();
    this.dirtyWars = true;
  }
  /** P3 joinFaction: into every ongoing war a faction member fights, against the other side's active members. */
  onFactionJoined(id: number, tick: number): void {
    const fid = this.ww.politics.factionIdx(id);
    if (fid < 0) return;
    for (const w of [...this.wars]) {
      if (w.ended !== null) continue;
      for (const s of [0, 1] as const) {
        if (!w.sides[s].members.some((m) => m.id !== id && m.state === "active" && this.ww.politics.factionIdx(m.id) === fid)) continue;
        for (const e of w.sides[1 - s].members) {
          if (e.state !== "active" || this.ww.isAtWar(id, e.id)) continue;
          this.ww.setWar(id, e.id, tick, { kind: "into", warId: w.id });
        }
      }
    }
  }
  setMemberState(warId: number, id: number, state: MemberState): void {
    const w = this.war(warId);
    const m = w === null ? null : this.member(w, id);
    if (m === null || w === null) return;
    m.state = state;
    this.fixLeader(w);
    this.dirtyWars = true;
  }
  /** Spec §4.3 last row: an inactive leader passes to the active member with most factories (ties smallID). */
  fixLeader(w: WwWar): void {
    for (const side of w.sides) {
      if (side.members.some((m) => m.id === side.leader && m.state === "active")) continue;
      let best = 0;
      let bestF = -1;
      for (const m of side.members) {
        if (m.state !== "active") continue;
        const f = this.factoriesOf(m.id);
        if (f > bestF || (f === bestF && m.id < best)) {
          best = m.id;
          bestF = f;
        }
      }
      if (best !== 0) side.leader = best;
    }
  }
  factoriesOf(id: number): number {
    const ce = this.ww.economy.country(id);
    return ce === null ? 0 : ce.totals.civ + ce.totals.mil;
  }
  private rebuildWarIdx(): void {
    this.warsOfIdx.clear();
    for (const w of this.wars) {
      for (const s of w.sides) for (const m of s.members) {
        const l = this.warsOfIdx.get(m.id);
        if (l === undefined) this.warsOfIdx.set(m.id, [w.id]);
        else l.push(w.id);
      }
    }
  }
  private dropOldWars(day: number): void {
    const kept = this.wars.filter((w) => w.ended === null || day - w.ended.day <= WW_WAR_KEEP_DAYS
      || this.conferences.some((c) => c.status !== 2 && c.warIds.includes(w.id)));
    if (kept.length !== this.wars.length) {
      this.wars = kept;
      this.rebuildWarIdx();
      this.dirtyWars = true;
    }
  }

  // ---- Countries, actors, controllers (plan decisions 6, 7) ----

  countryState(id: number): CountryState {
    return COUNTRY_STATES[this.countryStateArr[id] ?? CS_DORMANT];
  }
  setCountryState(id: number, s: number): void {
    if (this.countryStateArr[id] === s) return;
    this.countryStateArr[id] = s;
    this.dirtyCountries.add(id);
  }
  isActor(id: number): boolean {
    if (this.exileIdx.has(id)) return true;
    if (this.countryStateArr[id] !== CS_ACTIVE || this.ww.isCapitulated(id)) return false;
    const p = this.mg.playerBySmallID(id);
    return p.isPlayer() && p.isAlive();
  }
  isHumanControlled(id: number): boolean {
    return this.humanOf.has(id);
  }
  humanClientOf(id: number): string | null {
    return this.humanOf.get(id) ?? null;
  }
  controlledBy(client: string): number | null {
    for (const [c, id] of this.controllerOf) if (c === client) return id;
    return null;
  }
  /** Takeover (spec §11.3) and spectating (0). */
  setControl(client: string, smallID: number): void {
    this.controllerOf = this.controllerOf.filter(([c]) => c !== client);
    this.controllerOf.push([client, smallID]);
    this.controllerOf.sort((a, b) => (a[0] < b[0] ? -1 : a[0] > b[0] ? 1 : 0));
    this.rebuildHumans();
    this.dirtyControllers = true;
  }
  private rebuildHumans(): void {
    this.humanOf.clear();
    const moved = new Set(this.controllerOf.map(([c]) => c));
    for (const p of this.mg.allPlayers()) {
      const c = p.clientID();
      if (p.type() === PlayerType.Human && c !== null && !moved.has(c)) this.humanOf.set(p.smallID(), c);
    }
    for (const [c, id] of this.controllerOf) if (id > 0) this.humanOf.set(id, c);
  }
  displayNameKey(id: number): string | null {
    return this.nameKey[id];
  }

  // ---- Exiles, subjects, side (plan decision 20) ----

  isExile(id: number): boolean {
    return this.exileIdx.has(id);
  }
  exileHost(id: number): number {
    const i = this.exileIdx.get(id);
    return i === undefined ? 0 : this.exiles[i].host;
  }
  /** P2 side owner: an exile fights as its host. */
  sideOwner(id: number): number {
    const h = this.exileHost(id);
    return h > 0 ? h : id;
  }
  subjectOf(id: number): { overlord: number; level: SubjectLevel; neutral: boolean } | null {
    const i = this.subjectIdx.get(id);
    if (i === undefined) return null;
    const s = this.subjects[i];
    return { overlord: s.overlord, level: SUBJECT_LEVELS[s.level], neutral: s.neutral };
  }
  subjectsOf(overlord: number): number[] {
    return this.subjects.filter((s) => s.overlord === overlord).map((s) => s.subject).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  }

  // ---- Cores (spec §7, plan decision 17; foundation bodies) ----

  coreHolders(region: number): number[] {
    const out: number[] = [];
    for (let k = 0; k < WW_CORE_SLOTS; k++) {
      const v = this.coreTable[region * WW_CORE_SLOTS + k];
      if (v !== 0) out.push(v);
    }
    return out;
  }
  hasCore(id: number, region: number): boolean {
    if (id <= 0 || region <= 0 || region >= this.regionCap) return false;
    for (let k = 0; k < WW_CORE_SLOTS; k++) if (this.coreTable[region * WW_CORE_SLOTS + k] === id) return true;
    return false;
  }
  /** Spec §7.2: full slots push out the smallest-smallID gone (dormant first) holder; none gone → no add. */
  addCore(id: number, region: number): void {
    if (id <= 0 || region <= 0 || region >= this.regionCap || this.hasCore(id, region)) return;
    let h = this.coreHolders(region);
    if (h.length >= WW_CORE_SLOTS) {
      const out = h.filter((x) => this.countryStateArr[x] !== CS_ACTIVE && !this.isExile(x)).sort((a, b) => a - b)[0];
      if (out === undefined) return;
      h = h.filter((x) => x !== out);
    }
    h.push(id);
    this.writeCores(region, h);
  }
  removeCore(id: number, region: number): void {
    if (!this.hasCore(id, region)) return;
    this.writeCores(region, this.coreHolders(region).filter((x) => x !== id));
  }
  private writeCores(region: number, h: number[]): void {
    h.sort((a, b) => a - b);
    for (let k = 0; k < WW_CORE_SLOTS; k++) this.coreTable[region * WW_CORE_SLOTS + k] = h[k] ?? 0;
    this.dirtyCores.add(region);
    this.adminDirty = true;
  }

  // ---- Occupation queries (spec §8.1; O fills the shares) ----

  /** Spec §8.1: the controller holds no core there and someone else does. */
  computeAdministered(region: number): boolean {
    const c = this.ww.land.controller[region] ?? 0;
    if (c === 0 || this.hasCore(c, region)) return false;
    return this.coreHolders(region).some((x) => x !== c);
  }
  isAdministered(region: number): boolean {
    if (this.adminDirty) this.rebuildAdmin();
    return this.adminSet.has(region);
  }
  administeredOf(controller: number): readonly number[] {
    if (this.adminDirty) this.rebuildAdmin();
    return this.adminByController.get(controller) ?? [];
  }
  /** Called by P2 setController (foundation hook) and by core changes. */
  markControlChanged(region: number): void {
    this.adminDirty = true;
    this.dirtyOcc.add(region);
  }
  rebuildAdmin(): void {
    this.adminSet.clear();
    this.adminByController.clear();
    for (const r of this.ww.scenario.landRegions) {
      if (!this.computeAdministered(r.id)) {
        if (this.occSince[r.id] !== 0 || this.resBp[r.id] !== 0 || this.compBp[r.id] !== 0) this.clearOcc(r.id);
        continue;
      }
      this.adminSet.add(r.id);
      const c = this.ww.land.controller[r.id];
      const l = this.adminByController.get(c);
      if (l === undefined) this.adminByController.set(c, [r.id]);
      else l.push(r.id);
    }
    this.adminDirty = false;
  }
  clearOcc(region: number): void {
    this.resBp[region] = 0;
    this.compBp[region] = 0;
    this.occSince[region] = 0;
    this.compHigh[region] = 0;
    this.uprDays[region] = 0;
    this.supDeci[region] = 0;
    this.dirtyOcc.add(region);
  }
  /** P1 hook: null = the P1 rule (not administered). */
  regionShare(region: number): { factoryPct: number; resourcePct: number; manpowerPct: number } | null {
    return this.isAdministered(region) ? regionShareOf(this, region) : null;
  }
  /** P2 supply hook, permille (spec §8.6). */
  supplyFactorPm(region: number): number {
    return this.isAdministered(region) ? supplyFactorOf(this, region) : 1000;
  }
  occupationLawOf(by: number, of: number): OccupationLaw {
    const e = this.occLaw.find((x) => x.by === by && x.of === of) ?? this.occLaw.find((x) => x.by === by && x.of === 0);
    return e === undefined ? "occ_military" : OCCUPATION_LAWS[e.law];
  }
  isZone(region: number): boolean {
    return this.zones.some((z) => z.region === region);
  }
  zoneHolder(region: number): number {
    return this.zones.find((z) => z.region === region)?.holder ?? 0;
  }
  private expireZones(day: number): void {
    const kept = this.zones.filter((z) => z.untilDay < 0 || z.untilDay > day);
    if (kept.length !== this.zones.length) {
      this.zones = kept;
      this.dirtyZones = true;
    }
  }
  isDmz(region: number, smallID: number): boolean {
    return this.dmz.some(([r, of]) => r === region && of === smallID);
  }
  private expireTimed(day: number): void {
    this.reparations = this.reparations.filter((x) => x.untilDay > day);
    this.disarm = this.disarm.filter(([, until]) => until > day);
  }

  // ---- Hooks the earlier phases read (foundation inserts the calls; lanes fill the bodies) ----

  /** P1 refreshTotals (plan decision 19): what flows in and out today. */
  econAdjust(id: number): { res: number[]; civIn: number; civOut: number; manpower: number; virtualMil: number } {
    const out = { res: new Array<number>(this.ww.economy.resourceCount()).fill(0), civIn: 0, civOut: 0, manpower: 0, virtualMil: 0 };
    subjectEconomy(this, id, out);      // S
    reparationsEconomy(this, id, out);  // W
    exileEconomy(this, id, out);        // X
    return out;
  }
  /** P2 train / P4 naval lines, dock builds (spec §6.4 disarm). */
  disarmBlock(id: number, what: "train" | "naval" | "dock"): WwOrderBlock | null {
    return disarmBlockOf(this, id, what);  // W
  }
  /** P3 daily political power, percent (spec §10.4: 50..100 for an exile). */
  ppFactorPct(id: number): number {
    return this.isExile(id) ? exilePpFactorPct(this, id) : 100;  // X
  }
  /** P3 order handlers (spec §9.1). */
  subjectBlock(id: number, action: "declare" | "justify" | "faction" | "treaty" | "leave_faction"): boolean {
    return subjectBlock(this, id, action);  // S
  }
  /** P1 trade: percent of the civilian-factory cost (spec §9.1 dominion −50%). */
  tradeCivFactorPct(buyer: number, seller: number): number {
    return tradeCivFactorPct(this, buyer, seller);  // S
  }
  /** P2 canEnter for an army with volunteerFor (spec §12.2). */
  volunteerCanEnter(army: number, region: number): boolean {
    return volunteerCanEnter(this, army, region);  // A
  }
  /** P3 events: after an option's effects (plan decision 38). */
  onEventOption(eventId: string, optionId: string, self: number, from: number, tick: number): void {
    aidEventOption(this, eventId, optionId, self, from, tick);  // A
  }

  // ---- News, messages, daily notes ----

  news(id: string, params: Record<string, string | number>): void {
    this.endgameNews.push({ id, params });
  }
  message(to: number, key: string, params: Record<string, string | number>): void {
    if (!this.isHumanControlled(to)) return;
    const p = this.mg.playerBySmallID(to);
    if (!p.isPlayer()) return;
    this.mg.displayMessage(`events_display.${key}`, MessageType.WORLD_WAR, p.id(), undefined, params);
  }
  messageAll(key: string, params: Record<string, string | number>): void {
    this.mg.displayMessage(`events_display.${key}`, MessageType.WORLD_WAR, null, undefined, params);
  }
  note(to: number, kind: number, a: number, b: number, n: number): void {
    const e = this.dayNotes.find((x) => x[0] === to && x[1] === kind && x[2] === a && x[3] === b);
    if (e !== undefined) e[4] += n;
    else this.dayNotes.push([to, kind, a, b, n]);
  }
  /** One line per (country, kind) a day (spec §20.8). The lanes give the message text through NOTE_KEYS. */
  private flushDayNotes(id: number): void {
    const mine = this.dayNotes.filter((x) => x[0] === id);
    if (mine.length === 0) return;
    this.dayNotes = this.dayNotes.filter((x) => x[0] !== id);
    for (const kind of [0, 1, 2]) {
      const rows = mine.filter((x) => x[1] === kind);
      if (rows.length === 0) continue;
      const items = rows.map((x) => `${x[2]}:${x[3]}:${x[4]}`).join(",");
      this.message(id, NOTE_KEYS[kind], { items });
    }
  }

  // ---- Screens, snapshot, hash: Step 9 ----
  // takeData(), snapshot(), restoreState(s), rebuildDerived(), hash()
}

/** Plan decision 24: the three daily batches. `items` is "a:b:n,…" (the screen formats it). */
export const NOTE_KEYS = ["ww1936_captured", "ww1936_ll_arrived", "ww1936_garrison_short"] as const;
export const NOTE_CAPTURE = 0;
export const NOTE_LL = 1;
export const NOTE_GARRISON = 2;
```

`resourceCount()`는 P1 `WwEconomy`에 기반이 더하는 한 줄(`RESOURCE_COUNT`를 돌려준다). `PlayerType`, `MessageType`, `startSubjects`(Subjects.ts의 스텁 — 기반 본문이 spec §9.4 표를 그대로 넣는다: 캐나다·오스트레일리아·뉴질랜드·남아프리카 → 영국 `dominion` 6,000, 영국령 인도 → 영국 `colony` 4,000, 만주국 → 일본 `puppet` 2,000, 몽골 → 소련 `puppet` 5,000, 탄누투바 → 소련 `puppet` 3,000; 시나리오에 없는 태그는 건너뜀)를 import한다.

- [ ] **Step 8: 갈래 스텁 (파일 하나에 하나씩, 시그니처 고정)** (spec §25.3)

모든 스텁은 컴파일되고 **중립 값**(아무것도 안 함, `null` 거절 없음, 빈 배열)을 돌려준다. 예외는 표에 적었다(기존 테스트가 돌도록 앞 단계 동작을 흉내 내는 것).

| 파일(갈래) | 시그니처 | 기반 스텁의 동작 |
|---|---|---|
| `Wars.ts` (W) | `checkWarEnds(eg, day, tick): void`, `addWarScore(eg, warId, smallID, milli): void`, `noteDamage(eg, by, against, men): void`, `noteKills(eg, by, against, kind: "ship" \| "convoy" \| "plane", amount): void`, `dailyWarScore(eg, day): void`, `reparationsEconomy(eg, id, out): void`, `disarmBlockOf(eg, id, what): WwOrderBlock \| null`, `warScoreShare(eg, smallID, ofSmallID): number`(‰) | `addWarScore`만 본문(그 전쟁의 그 회원 `wsMilli += milli`, `dirtyWars`); 나머지 중립(`warScoreShare` 0) |
| `Capitulate.ts` (W) | `capitulate(eg, loser, victor, tick): void` | **P0~P4 흉내**: `endWarsOf(loser)`(회원 상태 `capitulated`를 먼저), 패전국 통제 주 → 승자(`transferOnCapitulation`), 법적 주인 → 승자(`setLegalOwner(r, victor)`), X `capitulationForces(eg, loser, victor, 0, tick)`(그 스텁이 P2 `removeCountryForces` + P4 `navy`·`air.onCapitulation`), P3 `onCapitulated`, 나라 상태 `gone` |
| `Conference.ts` (W) | `openConference(eg, warIds, tick): number`, `stepConferences(eg, tick): void`, `submitDemands(eg, smallID, conf, demands: readonly PeaceDemand[] \| null, tick): WwOrderBlock \| null`, `conferenceView(eg, conf): WwConferenceView` | 중립(`openConference` 0, `submitDemands` `"not_participant"`) |
| `PeaceAi.ts` (W) | `aiDemands(eg, conf, smallID): StoredDemand[]` | `[]` |
| `Occupation.ts` (O) | `stepOccupation(eg, controller, day)`, `regionShareOf(eg, region)`, `supplyFactorOf(eg, region): number`, `setOccupationLaw(eg, by, of, law, day)`, `integrateRegion(eg, smallID, region, day)`, `garrisonOrder(eg, smallID, army, scope)`, `uprisingNow(eg, region, tick): void`, `occView(eg, region): WwOccView` | `regionShareOf` = 점령은 `{50, 50, 0}`, 남의 코어를 가져간 땅은 `{100, 100, 10}`(P1 §4.4와 같은 값); `supplyFactorOf` 1000; `garrisonOrder`는 P2 `issueArmyOrder`에 `garrison`(범위 `enemy`)을 넣기만 |
| `Garrison.ts` (O) | `assignGarrisons(eg, owner, day)`, `supplyOf(eg, region): number`(0.1) | 중립 |
| `AiOccupation.ts` (O) | `aiOccupationDay(eg, id, day, tick)` | 중립 |
| `Subjects.ts` (S) | `startSubjects(eg)`, `stepSubject(eg, subject, day)`, `makeSubject(eg, subject, overlord, level \| "up" \| "down", neutral, tick)`, `endSubject(eg, subject, tick)`, `subjectEconomy(eg, id, out)`, `onOverlordWar(eg, overlord, enemy, defensive, tick)`, `endIndependenceWar(eg, warId, winner, tick)`, `subjectBlock(eg, id, action): boolean`, `tradeCivFactorPct(eg, buyer, seller): number`, `applySubjectEffect(ctx, e, actor)` | `startSubjects`·`makeSubject`·`endSubject`는 기록만(표·단계·자치도, 진영 묶기 없음), `subjectBlock` false, `tradeCivFactorPct` 100, 나머지 중립 |
| `AiSubjects.ts` (S) | `aiSubjectsDay(eg, id, day, tick)` | 중립 |
| `Activate.ts` (X) | `activateCountry(eg, tag, opts, tick): number`, `landRegained(eg, id, tick): void`, `applyActivationEffect(ctx, e, actor)`, `releasableTags(eg, stake): CountryTag[]` | `activateCountry`: 주 이양(`setLegalOwner` + `transferRegionPeaceful`), 상태 `active`, `clearCapitulation`, `wakeQueue`에 넣기, smallID 반환(그 밖 경제·군·정치 시작은 X); `landRegained` 본문(상태 `active`, `clearCapitulation`, `wakeQueue`) |
| `Exile.ts` (X) | `formExile(eg, loser, victor, tick): boolean`, `pickHost(eg, loser, victor): number`, `capitulationForces(eg, loser, victor, host, tick): void`, `stepExile(eg, id, day)`, `endExile(eg, id, tick, reason: "returned" \| "collapsed")`, `exileEconomy(eg, id, out)`, `exilePpFactorPct(eg, id): number`, `offerTakeover(eg, client, fromSmallID, tick)`, `resolveTakeovers(eg, tick)`, `takeoverPick(eg, smallID, pick, tick)` | `formExile` false, `pickHost` 0; `capitulationForces` = P2 `removeCountryForces` + P4 `navy`·`air.onCapitulation`(P0~P4 흉내); `exilePpFactorPct` 100; `offerTakeover`는 관전(`setControl(client, 0)`, `ww1936_spectating`); 나머지 중립 |
| `Takeover.ts` (X) | `takeoverCandidates(eg, fromSmallID): number[]` | `[]` |
| `Liberation.ts` (X) | `returnLiberated(eg, day, tick)` | 중립 |
| `LendLease.ts` (A) | `stepLendLease(eg, sender, day)`, `deliverLendLease(eg, day)`, `issueAid(eg, smallID, order, tick)`, `aidEventOption(eg, eventId, optionId, self, from, tick)`, `volunteerCanEnter(eg, army, region): boolean`, `applyAidEffect(ctx, e, actor)`, `aidView(eg, id): WwAidView` | `issueAid` = `"ll_conditions"`, `volunteerCanEnter` false, `aidView`은 본문(계약·운송·의용군 목록 그대로), 나머지 중립 |
| `Volunteers.ts` (A) | `sendVolunteers(eg, army, to, tick)`, `recallVolunteers(eg, army, tick)`, `sendAirVolunteers(eg, from, to, zone, plane, count, tick)`, `transferShips(eg, from, to, ships, tick)` | 거절 `"volunteer_conditions"` / 중립 |
| `Capture.ts` (A) | `captureOnBattleEnd(eg, battle: Battle, winnerSide: 0 \| 1): void`, `captureOnEncircled(eg, row: number, by: number): void`, `captureOnCapitulation(eg, loser, victor): void` | 중립 |
| `AiAid.ts` (A) | `aiAidDay(eg, id, day, tick)`, `llRequestScore(eg, from, to, item): number` | 중립 / 0 |
| `CivilWar.ts` (C) | `startCivilWar(eg, smallID, ideology, tick): boolean`, `endCivilWar(eg, warId, winner: 0 \| 1, tick): void` | `false`(P3 임시 처리로) |
| `EffectsEndgame.ts` (E, 분배는 기반) | `applyEndgameEffect(ctx, e, actor)` | 기반 본문: `switch(e.kind)`에서 `release`·`create_state`·`restore_country`·`set_name` → `applyActivationEffect`(X), `make_subject`·`end_subject`·`autonomy` → `applySubjectEffect`(S), `lend_lease`·`volunteers`·`transfer_ships`·`scuttle_fleet`·`embargo`·`end_embargo` → `applyAidEffect`(A), 나머지 → `applyOtherEndgameEffect(ctx, e, actor)`(E 스텁: 중립) |
| `ConditionsEndgame.ts` (E) | `evalEndgameCondition(ctx, c): boolean` | `false` |
| `Executions.ts` (기반 본문) | `addCountryExecutions(mg, p, gameID): void`, `wakePending(eg, tick): void` | 결정 5 그대로(본문) |

`applyAidEffect(ctx, e, actor)`는 `LendLease.ts`(A)에 있다. 스텁 파일 맨 위에 `// P5 lane <글자> fills this file (plan Task <n>)`.

- [ ] **Step 9: 화면 데이터·스냅샷·해시·파생** (spec §21.4~§21.5, 결정 18, 23, 25)

`WwEndgame` 안(Step 7 클래스의 끝):

```ts
  takeData(): WwEndgameData | null {
    const first = this.firstData;
    const d: WwEndgameData = { news: this.endgameNews };
    if (first || this.dirtyCountries.size > 0) {
      const ids = first ? [...Array(this.size - 1).keys()].map((i) => i + 1) : [...this.dirtyCountries].sort((a, b) => a - b);
      d.countries = ids.map((id) => ({
        smallID: id, state: this.countryState(id), nameKey: this.nameKey[id],
        nameArg: (this.nameArg[id] as CountryTag | null) ?? null,
        color: this.color[id] < 0 ? null : this.color[id], flag: this.flagOf(id),
      }));
    }
    if (first || this.dirtyControllers) d.controllers = this.controllerOf.map(([c, i]) => [c, i]);
    if (first || this.dirtyWars) d.wars = this.wars.map((w) => warSummary(this, w));
    if (first || this.dirtyConf) {
      const open = this.conferences.find((c) => c.status === 1 && c.parts.some((p) => p.human)) ?? null;
      d.conference = open === null ? null : conferenceView(this, open.id);
      d.queuedConferences = this.conferences.filter((c) => c.status === 0).length;
    }
    if (first || this.dirtyPeaceLog) d.peaceLog = this.peaceLog;
    if (this.dirtyOcc.size > 0) {
      d.occupation = [...this.dirtyOcc].sort((a, b) => a - b)
        .filter((r) => this.isAdministered(r) && (this.isHumanControlled(this.ww.land.controller[r]) || this.coreHolders(r).some((h) => this.isHumanControlled(h))))
        .map((r) => occView(this, r));
      d.occLaws = this.occLaw.map((x) => ({ by: x.by, of: x.of, law: OCCUPATION_LAWS[x.law], lockUntilDay: x.lockUntilDay }));
    }
    if (this.dirtyCores.size > 0) d.coreChanges = [...this.dirtyCores].sort((a, b) => a - b).map((r) => ({ region: r, holders: this.coreHolders(r) }));
    if (first || this.dirtyZones) d.zones = this.zones.map((z) => ({ ...z }));
    if (first || this.dirtySubjects) d.subjects = this.subjects.map((s) => ({
      subject: s.subject, overlord: s.overlord, level: SUBJECT_LEVELS[s.level], autBp: s.autBp, deltaBp: s.lastDelta, neutral: s.neutral,
    }));
    if (first || this.dirtyExiles) d.exiles = this.exiles.map((e) => ({
      smallID: e.smallID, host: e.host, legitBp: e.legitBp, deltaBp: e.lastDelta, virtualMil: Math.floor(e.legitBp / 1000), capital: e.capital,
    }));
    if (this.dirtyAid.size > 0) d.aid = [...this.dirtyAid].sort((a, b) => a - b).filter((id) => this.isHumanControlled(id)).map((id) => aidView(this, id));
    if (first || this.dirtyTakeover) d.takeover = this.takeoverCards.map((c) => ({ client: c.client, from: c.from, candidates: [...c.candidates], deadlineTick: c.deadlineTick }));
    const empty = Object.keys(d).length === 1 && d.news.length === 0;
    this.endgameNews = [];
    this.firstData = false;
    this.dirtyCountries.clear(); this.dirtyWars = false; this.dirtyConf = false; this.dirtyPeaceLog = false;
    this.dirtyCores.clear(); this.dirtyOcc.clear(); this.dirtySubjects = false; this.dirtyExiles = false;
    this.dirtyZones = false; this.dirtyAid.clear(); this.dirtyControllers = false; this.dirtyTakeover = false;
    return empty ? null : d;
  }

  flagOf(id: number): string {
    const tag = this.ww.tagOf(id);
    if (this.nameKey[id] !== null && tag.startsWith("CW")) return `ww_cw_${this.ww.politics.country(id)?.rulingName() ?? "neutral"}`;
    return COUNTRIES[tag].flag;
  }

  snapshot(): WwEndgameState {
    const cores: [number, number, number, number][] = [];
    for (let r = 1; r < this.regionCap; r++) {
      const h = this.coreHolders(r);
      if (h.length > 0) cores.push([r, h[0] ?? 0, h[1] ?? 0, h[2] ?? 0]);
    }
    const occ: [number, number, number, number, number, number, number][] = [];
    for (let r = 1; r < this.regionCap; r++) {
      if (this.resBp[r] | this.compBp[r] | this.occSince[r] | this.compHigh[r] | this.uprDays[r] | this.supDeci[r]) {
        occ.push([r, this.resBp[r], this.compBp[r], this.occSince[r], this.compHigh[r], this.uprDays[r], this.supDeci[r]]);
      }
    }
    return structuredCloneLite({
      gameID: this.gameID, lastCommonDay: this.lastCommonDay,
      countryState: [...this.countryStateArr], nameKey: this.nameKey, nameArg: this.nameArg, color: this.color,
      controllerOf: this.controllerOf, everHuman: this.everHuman,
      wars: this.wars, nextWarId: this.nextWarId,
      pairWar: [...this.pairWar].sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]).map(([k, w]) => [Math.floor(k / this.size), k % this.size, w]),
      conferences: this.conferences, nextConfId: this.nextConfId, peaceLog: this.peaceLog,
      cores, occ, occLaw: this.occLaw, zones: this.zones, dmz: this.dmz, subjects: this.subjects, exiles: this.exiles,
      lendLease: this.lendLease, inTransit: this.inTransit, nextLlId: this.nextLlId, volunteers: this.volunteers,
      reparations: this.reparations, disarm: this.disarm, takeoverCards: this.takeoverCards,
      wakeQueue: this.wakeQueue, dayNotes: this.dayNotes,
    });
  }

  restoreState(s: WwEndgameState): void {
    const c = structuredCloneLite(s);
    this.gameID = c.gameID;
    this.lastCommonDay = c.lastCommonDay;
    this.countryStateArr = Uint8Array.from(c.countryState);
    this.nameKey = c.nameKey; this.nameArg = c.nameArg; this.color = c.color;
    this.controllerOf = c.controllerOf; this.everHuman = c.everHuman;
    this.wars = c.wars as WwWar[]; this.nextWarId = c.nextWarId;
    this.pairWar = new Map(c.pairWar.map(([a, b, w]) => [WwEndgame.pairKey(a, b, this.size), w]));
    this.conferences = c.conferences; this.nextConfId = c.nextConfId; this.peaceLog = c.peaceLog;
    this.coreTable.fill(0);
    for (const [r, a, b, d] of c.cores) this.writeCoresRaw(r, [a, b, d].filter((x) => x !== 0));
    for (const arr of [this.resBp, this.compBp, this.occSince, this.compHigh, this.uprDays, this.supDeci]) arr.fill(0);
    for (const [r, res, comp, since, high, upr, sup] of c.occ) {
      this.resBp[r] = res; this.compBp[r] = comp; this.occSince[r] = since;
      this.compHigh[r] = high; this.uprDays[r] = upr; this.supDeci[r] = sup;
    }
    this.occLaw = c.occLaw; this.zones = c.zones; this.dmz = c.dmz; this.subjects = c.subjects; this.exiles = c.exiles;
    this.lendLease = c.lendLease; this.inTransit = c.inTransit; this.nextLlId = c.nextLlId; this.volunteers = c.volunteers;
    this.reparations = c.reparations; this.disarm = c.disarm; this.takeoverCards = c.takeoverCards;
    this.wakeQueue = c.wakeQueue; this.dayNotes = c.dayNotes;
    this.rebuildDerived();
  }

  /** Everything in DERIVED_FIELDS, from state only. */
  rebuildDerived(): void {
    this.rebuildHumans();
    this.rebuildWarIdx();
    this.subjectIdx = new Map(this.subjects.map((s, i) => [s.subject, i]));
    this.exileIdx = new Map(this.exiles.map((e, i) => [e.smallID, i]));
    this.adminDirty = true;
    this.rebuildAdmin();
  }

  /** Spec §21.5. */
  hash(): number {
    let h = 0;
    const mix = (v: number) => { h = (Math.imul(h, 31) + (v | 0)) | 0; };
    for (let i = 1; i < this.size; i++) mix(this.countryStateArr[i] * (i + 1));
    mix(this.wars.length);
    for (const w of this.wars) for (const s of w.sides) for (const m of s.members) mix(MEMBER_STATES.indexOf(m.state) + m.id * 4 + (m.wsMilli % 1_000_003));
    for (const c of this.conferences) { mix(c.round); mix(c.settled.length); mix(c.status); }
    for (const r of this.adminSet) { mix(this.resBp[r]); mix(this.compBp[r]); }
    for (const s of this.subjects) { mix(s.level); mix(s.autBp); }
    for (const e of this.exiles) mix(e.legitBp);
    for (const l of this.lendLease) mix(l.sentMilli % 1_000_003);
    for (const t of this.inTransit) mix(t.countMilli % 1_000_003);
    for (let i = 0; i < this.coreTable.length; i += 97) mix(this.coreTable[i]);
    for (const [c, id] of this.controllerOf) { mix(c.length); mix(id); }
    return h;
  }
```

`structuredCloneLite`는 `engine-lib/snapshot`의 깊은 복사 도우미(P3·P4가 쓰는 것과 같은 이름; 없으면 `JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x))`를 이 파일 안 함수로). `writeCoresRaw`는 `writeCores`에서 더럽힘·정렬을 뺀 것. `warSummary`(W `Wars.ts` — 기반 본문: 편마다 회원과 `sharePm`(그 편의 `wsMilli` 합 중 비율)), `occView`(O 스텁: 상태 숫자만 채운 줄), `aidView`(A `LendLease.ts` — 기반 본문: 계약·운송·의용군 목록을 그대로), `conferenceView`(W 스텁: 최소 모양)는 그 갈래 파일에 있다. `rulingName()`은 P3 `CountryPol`의 집권 이념 이름(코드 이름이 다르면 그것).

`tests/util/Snapshot.ts`의 `DERIVED_FIELDS`에 `humanOf`, `adminByController`, `adminSet`, `adminDirty`, `subjectIdx`, `exileIdx`, `warsOfIdx`, `endgameNews`, `dirtyCountries`, `dirtyWars`, `dirtyConf`, `dirtyPeaceLog`, `dirtyCores`, `dirtyOcc`, `dirtySubjects`, `dirtyExiles`, `dirtyZones`, `dirtyAid`, `dirtyControllers`, `dirtyTakeover`, `firstData`. `pairWar`는 상태(스냅샷에 배열로)이지만 오라클은 `Map`을 그대로 비교한다.

`WorldWarStateSchema`에 `endgame: WwEndgameStateSchema.optional()`; `WorldWar.snapshot()`이 `endgame: this.endgame.snapshot()`, `restoreState`는 없으면 `throw new Error("snapshot without the P5 endgame state")`(결정 25).

- [ ] **Step 10: P0 연결** (spec §16.1, 결정 3~9)

- `ScenarioIndex`: `countries` = 시나리오 태그(지금 그대로) + `DORMANT_TAGS`. `scenarioTags()`(앞부분 `slice(0, scenarioCount)`), `scenarioCount`. `capitals`·`capitalTile(tag)`·`capitalXY(tag)`는 잠든 태그면 `undefined`/예외 대신 **수도 없음**을 돌려준다(`capitalTileOrNull`; 기존 함수는 시나리오 태그만 받는다고 주석). `startVpBySmallId()`는 잠든 칸 0.
- `ScenarioPlayers.scenarioPlayers`: 시나리오 태그 다음에 잠든 칸 `PlayerInfo`(결정 4). `assignCountries(players, scenario.scenarioTags())`.
- `WorldWar`: `readonly endgame: WwEndgame`(결정 2), `start()`의 스폰 타일 줄은 `isDormantTag(tag)`면 건너뜀. 새 메서드: `isActor(id)`, `isHumanControlled(id)`, `controlledBy(client)`(모두 `endgame`에 넘김), `clearCapitulation(id)`(`_capitulated`에서 지우고 게이지 0, `dataDirty`), `setWar(a, b, tick, link: WarLink): boolean`(쌍을 넣은 뒤 `this.endgame.onWarPairCreated(a, b, link, tick)`), `endWar(a, b): boolean`(P3가 이미 만들었으면 그것에) 끝에 `endgame.onWarPairRemoved`, `endWarsOf(id)`는 지우는 쌍마다 `onWarPairRemoved`. `hash()` 끝에 `endgame.hash()`.
- `Diplomacy.declareWar(mg, ww, attacker, target, tick, opts?: {goal?, war?: WarKind, independence?: boolean})`: 첫 `setWar(a, target, tick, {kind: "declare", goal: opts.goal, war: opts.war})`(P3가 넘기는 목표가 있으면), 진영 연쇄 `setWar(a, m, tick, {kind: "into", warId: ww.endgame.warOfPair(a, target)})`. `joinWar`: `{kind: "into", warId: 0}`. `declareWarBlock` 끝에: 선포국이 `integrated`/`puppet` 속국이고 대상이 종주국의 적이 아니면 `"subject_restricted"`(괴뢰는 종주국의 적에게만), 선포국이 망명 정부면 `"exile"`(`opts.independence`면 속국 거절을 건너뛴다 — S의 독립 전쟁). `!attacker.isAlive() || !target.isAlive()`는 `!ww.isActor(a) || !ww.isActor(b)`로(망명 정부는 선포는 못 하지만 — 위 `exile` — 선포를 받을 수는 있다).
- `WW_INITIAL_WARS`·P3 비트 `declare`(P0 안전망 포함): `{kind: "declare", war: beat.civil ? "civil" : undefined}`.
- `Capitulation`: `checkCapitulations`의 `p.isAlive()` 검사는 그대로 두고 `if (ww.endgame.isExile(id)) { gauge 0; continue; }`를 맨 앞에. `capitulate`: `addCapitulation`·게이지·원작 공격 정리 다음의 P0 이양·`endWarsOf`·P2/P3/P4 이양 줄을 모두 지우고 `ww.endgame.onCapitulated(loserID, victor?.smallID() ?? 0, tick)` 한 줄(결정 9).
- `Victory.scoreInputs`: `dormant` 나라는 줄에서 뺀다. `faction`은 속국이고 자기 진영이 없으면 종주국의 진영(spec §16.1).
- `GameRunner.init`(모드 분기): `ww.endgame.setGameID(gameID)`를 먼저 부르고, `PlayerExecution`을 붙이는 줄은 잠든 칸을 건너뛴다(`if (isDormantTag(ww.tagOf(p.smallID()))) continue;`). `nationExecutions()` 줄은 그대로다(잠든 칸은 `mg.nations()`에 없다 — 결정 4). 실행 순서(모든 `PlayerExecution` 다음 모든 `NationExecution`)가 원작 그대로라 고정값은 잠든 칸의 `nextID`만큼만 바뀐다. `addCountryExecutions`(Step 13)는 **깨울 때만** 쓴다 — 결정 4의 "시작과 깨우기가 같이 쓴다"는 같은 실행 종류·같은 생성자 인자라는 뜻으로 읽는다.
- `GameImpl.playerByClientID`: 결정 7의 한 분기.
- `NationExecution.tick`: 결정 7의 한 줄.
- 서버 `GameServer.pickCountry`: `isDormantTag(tag)`면 거절(지금 잘못된 태그를 거절하는 길 그대로). 화면 `CountryPicker`: 목록을 `COUNTRY_TAGS.filter((t) => !isDormantTag(t))`로.

- [ ] **Step 11: P1·P2 연결** (spec §16.2~§16.3, 결정 6, 19~21)

- P1 `WwEconomy.stepCountry`: 첫 검사 `!p.isPlayer() || !p.isAlive() || this.ww.isCapitulated(id)` → `!this.ww.isActor(id)`. AI 문 `p.type() === PlayerType.Nation` → `!this.ww.isHumanControlled(id)`. `refreshTotals`: 주마다 `const occ = this.ww.endgame.regionShare(id); const sh = occ === null ? regionShares(…) : { controlPct: occ.factoryPct, manpowerPct: occ.manpowerPct, … };`(자원 추출도 `occ.resourcePct`), 끝에서 `const adj = this.ww.endgame.econAdjust(ce.smallID)`로 `produced[k] += adj.res[k]`(음수면 0 아래로 안 감), `received += adj.civIn`, `paid += adj.civOut`, `recruitable += adj.manpower`, `t.mil += adj.virtualMil`. `shares(region)`도 같은 분기. P3가 바꾼 `core` 인자는 `this.ww.endgame.hasCore(legalOwner, region)`(결정 17).
- P1 `WwOrderExecution.applyWwOrder`: 보낸 사람 검사 `isAlive` → `ww.isActor(smallID)`; `ENDGAME_ORDER_KINDS`면 `ww.endgame.issue`.
- P2 `WwLand`: (1) `rebuildDerived`의 편 열쇠: `sideKeyOf(id)` = `sideKeyOf(eg.sideOwner(id))`(결정 20). (2) `divisionSide(row)`(본문): `const a = this.armies.get(this.divisions.army[row]); const v = a?.volunteerFor ?? 0; return this.sideOf(v > 0 ? v : this.divisions.owner[row]);`. 전투 참가·적 판정·`canEnter`에서 사단의 편을 `sideOf(owner)`로 읽는 줄을 `divisionSide(row)`로. (3) `Army`에 `volunteerFor: number`(스키마 기본 0). (4) `Battle`에 `capEq: number[]`(결정 21; 장비 손실 자리에서 더함). 전투가 끝나는 자리에 `captureOnBattleEnd(ww.endgame, battle, winnerSide)`, 포위 섬멸 자리에 `captureOnEncircled(ww.endgame, row, by)`. (5) `setController` 끝에 `this.ww.endgame.markControlChanged(region)`. (6) `dailySupply`의 주 보급량에 `× eg.supplyFactorPm(region) / 1000`. (7) `issueArmyOrder`의 `garrison`: `{kind: "garrison", enemy: scope}`를 그대로 넣고 그 군의 `pending`을 세운다(목적지는 O가 정한다); 전선 배정·작전·AI 군 묶기는 `order.kind === "garrison"`인 군을 건너뛴다(`AiLand`, `Orders`의 전선 함수). (8) 훈련(`train`) 거절 앞에 `eg.disarmBlock(id, "train")`. (9) 새 `transferArmyTo(land, army, to)`와 `transferDivisionsTo(land, ids, to, armyNameNo?)`(`PoliticsBridge.ts`, 본문: P3 `transferCountryUnits`의 "군을 받는 나라의 새 군 번호로, 편제는 같은 이름 편제나 복사" 길을 군·사단 단위로 떼어 낸 것; `transferCountryUnits`는 이것을 군마다 부르게 바꾼다 — 동작 같음). (10) 수비대 공급을 위한 질의 `divisionsIn(region): number[]`(이미 `rowsIn`이 있으면 그것). (11) 사단 진압력은 `divisionSuppression(templateOf(row), strengthPm(row))`(engine-lib) — P2에 새 열 없음.
- P2 `damage`(사상자) 자리: 전투 라운드에서 피해를 준 사단마다 `by` = 그 군의 `volunteerFor || owner`로 `noteDamage(eg, by, againstOwner, men)`(W 스텁)와 `battle.dmgBy`에 더하기(결정 21). P2 `capture(land, region, by)`: 점령한 사단의 군에 `volunteerFor`가 있으면 `by`를 그 나라로. P2 `canEnter`: 군에 `volunteerFor`가 있으면 `eg.volunteerCanEnter(army, region)`이 먼저; 그 주가 `eg.isDmz(region, owner)`면 막음. P1 무역의 공장 비용 계산에 `× eg.tradeCivFactorPct(buyer, seller) / 100`(올림).

- [ ] **Step 12: P3·P4 연결** (spec §16.4~§16.5, 결정 6~9, 17, 22, 26)

- P3 `WwPolitics.living(id)` → `this.ww.isActor(id)`; `isHuman(id)` → `this.ww.isHumanControlled(id)`. `onCapitulated`의 법적 주인 이양 줄(`setLegalOwner(r.id, victor, false)`)을 지운다(spec §5.1-2: 법적 주인은 그대로). `setLegalOwner(region, to, core?)`: `core === true`면 `ww.endgame.addCore(to, region)`; `coreOverride` 필드·스키마·화면 표시를 지우고 화면은 `WwEndgame.coreChanges`를 읽는다(결정 17).
- P3 `civilWar(pol, id, ideology, tick)` 첫 줄: `if (startCivilWar(pol.ww.endgame, id, IDEOLOGIES[ideology], tick)) return;`.
- P3 `annexCountry`: 대상이 사람이 맡은 나라면 끝에서 `offerTakeover(eg, client, target, tick)`(X); 대상의 속국은 합병국의 속국으로(`makeSubject(eg, s, by, level, neutral, tick)` — S 스텁은 기록만); 대상 상태 `gone`.
- P3 `endWarBetween`: 쌍을 지우는 길이 `ww.endWar(a, b)`면 그것이 `onWarPairRemoved`를 부른다(Step 10).
- P3 `joinFaction`: 끝에 `ww.endgame.onFactionJoined(id, tick)`. 보장 발동·참전 요청 수락은 `joinWar`(→ `into`).
- P3 정치력 하루 몫 끝에 `× ww.endgame.ppFactorPct(id) / 100`. P3 명령 처리(`issue`) 맨 앞: 선포·정당화(`justify`), 진영(만들기·가입·초청·탈퇴), 조약(보장·불가침·통행) 종류마다 `ww.endgame.subjectBlock(id, action)`이 참이면 `"subject_restricted"`. P3 이벤트 선택지 적용 함수 끝에 `ww.endgame.onEventOption(event.id, option.id, self, from, tick)`. P3 `resolveSelector`에 `overlordOf`: `ww.endgame.subjectOf(resolveSelector(ctx, s.overlordOf))?.overlord ?? 0`.
- P3 `applyEffect`: `case "endgame": applyEndgameEffect(ctx, e, actor); break;`. `evalCondition`: `default: return evalEndgameCondition(ctx, c);`. P3 `HistoryBeats.runBeat`: `case "effects": applyEffects(makeCtx(pol, actor, actor, 0, tick), act.effects); break;`. P3 이벤트 카드: "사람에게 카드"의 판단은 이미 `pol.isHuman`(= `isHumanControlled`)이다.
- P3 `factionOf`: 망명 정부가 진영이 없고 망명지가 진영에 있으면 그 진영 — **상태로** 넣는다(X가 `formExile`에서 P3 `joinFaction`을 부른다; 기반은 아무것도 하지 않는다).
- P4 `WwNavy`: `lossRatePm(srIdx, owner): number`(결정 22), `navy.onCapitulation`은 그대로 두되 W의 `capitulate`만 부른다(Step 8). `NavalLines`의 라인 더하기·조선소 건설 거절 앞에 `eg.disarmBlock(id, "naval")`/`"dock"`. `CONVOY_USES`의 `lend_lease`는 P4 `allocateConvoys`가 `CONVOY_USE_PRIORITY` 순으로 이미 다룬다(경로 만들기는 A).
- P4 해전 격침(`removeShip(…, sunk: true)`을 부르는 자리), 통상 파괴 격침, 공중전 격추 자리에 `noteKills(eg, 이긴 쪽 나라, 잃은 쪽 나라, "ship" | "convoy" | "plane", 양)`(W 스텁; 함선은 우세 무게).
- P4 `WwAir`: `Deployment.volunteerFor: number`(기본 0, 스키마), `deploymentSide(dep)` = `land.sideOf(dep.volunteerFor > 0 ? dep.volunteerFor : dep.owner)`(본문), 공역의 편 계산이 배치 주인을 읽는 줄을 이것으로.
- 이 모드의 나머지 AI 문(P2 `AiLand`, P4 `AiNavy`·`AiAir`·`AiInvasion`, P1 `AiEconomy`)은 결정 7의 grep 결과대로 `isHumanControlled`; 나라 차례는 결정 6대로 `isActor`.

- [ ] **Step 13: 시계·명령·업데이트 연결** (spec §2.2, 결정 23)

`WorldWarExecution.tick`: P3 `politics.tick(ticks)` 다음 줄 `ww.endgame.clock(ticks);`, P4 `air.tick(ticks)` 다음 줄 `ww.endgame.tick(ticks);`, P4 업데이트 다음 `const eg = ww.endgame.takeData(); if (eg !== null) mg.addUpdate({ type: GameUpdateType.WwEndgame, data: eg });`. 준비 1분에는 `clock`이 하루 일을 하지 않고, `tick`(회의·넘겨받기 기한)은 돈다.

`addCountryExecutions`·`wakePending`(`Executions.ts`, 본문):

```ts
import { Cell, Nation } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";
import type { Game, Player } from "../../game/Game";
import { NationExecution } from "../../execution/NationExecution";
import { PlayerExecution } from "../../execution/PlayerExecution";
import type { WwEndgame } from "./WwEndgame";

/** Plan decision 5: the executions GameRunner.init gives a starting country; never twice. */
export function addCountryExecutions(mg: Game, p: Player, gameID: string): void {
  const live = mg.executions().filter((e) => e.isActive());
  if (!live.some((e) => e instanceof PlayerExecution && e.playerRef() === p)) mg.addExecution(new PlayerExecution(p));
  if (!mg.config().spawnNations()) return;
  if (live.some((e) => e instanceof NationExecution && e.playerRef() === p)) return;
  const tile = spawnTileOf(mg, p);
  if (tile === null) return;
  mg.addExecution(new NationExecution(gameID, new Nation(new Cell(mg.x(tile), mg.y(tile)), p.info())));
}

/** Spec §3.3-2: executions for countries woken this tick (order 8). */
export function wakePending(eg: WwEndgame, _tick: number): void {
  if (eg.wakeQueue.length === 0) return;
  const ids = [...new Set(eg.wakeQueue)].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  eg.wakeQueue = [];
  for (const id of ids) {
    const p = eg.mg.playerBySmallID(id);
    if (p.isPlayer() && p.isAlive()) addCountryExecutions(eg.mg, p, eg.gameID);
  }
}

function spawnTileOf(mg: Game, p: Player): number | null {
  const ww = mg.worldWar()!;
  let best = 0;
  let bestVp = -1;
  for (const r of ww.scenario.landRegions) {
    if (ww.land.controller[r.id] !== p.smallID()) continue;
    if (r.vp > bestVp) { best = r.id; bestVp = r.vp; }
  }
  return best === 0 ? null : ww.scenario.vpTile(ww.scenario.region(best)!);
}
```

`playerRef()`는 두 실행에 기반이 더하는 읽기 함수(원작 필드가 `private`이면); 원작 동작은 바뀌지 않는다. `p.info()`가 없으면 원작 `PlayerImpl`의 `PlayerInfo` 읽기 이름을 쓴다.

- [ ] **Step 14: 화면 연결 `src/client/view/WwEndgameView.ts`** (spec §3.5~§3.6, §21.4, 결정 23)

```ts
export class WwEndgameView {
  countries = new Map<number, WwCountryView>();
  controllers: [string, number][] = [];
  wars: WwWarSummary[] = [];
  conferenceView: WwConferenceView | null = null;
  queued = 0;
  peaceLog: WwPeaceLogEntry[] = [];
  occupation = new Map<number, WwOccView>();
  occLaws: { by: number; of: number; law: OccupationLaw; lockUntilDay: number }[] = [];
  cores = new Map<number, number[]>();
  zones: WwZoneView[] = [];
  subjects: WwSubjectView[] = [];
  exiles: WwExileView[] = [];
  aid = new Map<number, WwAidView>();
  takeover: WwTakeoverView[] = [];
  news: { id: string; params: Record<string, string | number> }[] = [];

  apply(d: WwEndgameData): void { /* merge every present field; occupation/aid/cores merge by key; news appended (last 30) */ }
  /** Spec §3.5: the map label and every country name on screen. */
  displayName(smallID: number, tagOf: (id: number) => CountryTag): string {
    const c = this.countries.get(smallID);
    if (c?.nameKey) return translateText(c.nameKey, c.nameArg ? { country: translateText(`ww1936.country.${c.nameArg}`) } : {});
    return translateText(`ww1936.country.${tagOf(smallID)}`);
  }
  controlledBy(client: string): number | null { /* controllers row; 0 = spectating */ }
  isExile(id: number): boolean { return this.exiles.some((e) => e.smallID === id); }
  subjectOf(id: number): WwSubjectView | null { return this.subjects.find((s) => s.subject === id) ?? null; }
  conference(): WwConferenceView | null { return this.conferenceView; }
  isDormant(id: number): boolean { return this.countries.get(id)?.state === "dormant"; }
  takeoverFor(client: string): WwTakeoverView | null { return this.takeover.find((t) => t.client === client) ?? null; }
}
```

`GameView`: `WwEndgame` 업데이트를 `wwEndgame.apply`로 받고, `myPlayer()`는 모드에서 `wwEndgame.controlledBy(myClientID)`가 숫자면 그 나라(0이면 `null` — 관전)를 돌려준다. 바뀐 틱에 `WwControllerChangedEvent`(새, `src/client/hud/ww1936/` 밖의 view 파일)를 띄워 열린 서랍을 닫게 한다(U3가 받는다). `PeaceModel`·`EndgameModel`은 빈 클래스 스텁(메서드 이름만: `PeaceModel.{remainingPts, previewCost, demandRows, stakeRegion, canRelease}`, `EndgameModel.{occupationSummary, byLegalOwner, aidRows, subjectRows, warCards}`).

`scripts/ww1936/make-test-map.ts --variant end`: `pol` 변형의 주 1~19를 그대로 만든 뒤 주 20과 간선 15–20을 더한다. `tests/testdata/maps/ww1936end/`에 `map.bin`, `regions.bin`, 미니맵, `scenario.json`. `tests/ww1936/util.ts`의 `wwRunner`에 `map: "ww1936end"` 선택지. `endFixture.ts`(`END`), `endgameFixture.ts`(위 표의 시험 콘텐츠, `politicsFixture({...extra, groups, cores, peace, events})`로).

- [ ] **Step 16: 기반 테스트** (spec §23.2 기반 줄, Review Focus 1·4·5)

- `EndgameTypes.test.ts`: 목록 길이와 순서(`DORMANT_TAGS` 14가 `COUNTRY_TAGS`의 맨 뒤 14와 같은 순서, `ENDGAME_ORDER_KINDS` 13 = `WwOrderSchema`에서 `air_auto` 뒤의 `kind`, `WW_ORDER_BLOCKS` 뒤 15, `WW_STAT_KEYS`의 `paradropPrepPct` 뒤 7, `EFFECT_KINDS`의 `fuel` 뒤 23, `CONDITION_KINDS`의 `fuel_ratio_below` 뒤 13, `effectGroup("release") === "endgame"`, `effectGroup("ships") === "navy"`, `effectGroup("annex") === "territory"`), `PICK_ORDER`에 잠든 태그 없음.
- `EndgameWiring.test.ts`: "dormant countries exist from tick 0 with no land and no executions"(`ww1936end`, 24명, 11~24 타일 0, 실행 목록에 그 플레이어의 `PlayerExecution`·`NationExecution` 없음, 순위표 입력·`scoreInputs`에 없음, `countryState` dormant), "a woken country gets its executions and economy on the tick its land arrives"(`activateCountry(eg, "SVK", {regions: [15, 20]}, t)`(기반 스텁 본문) → 같은 틱 끝에 SVK 타일 > 0, 승점 타일 주인 SVK, `PlayerExecution` 붙음, `countryState` active, 다음 P1 차례에 `CountryEconomy` 합계가 두 주의 공장을 셈), "a human command reaches the country the client now controls"(사람 POL, `eg.setControl(polClient, CSK)` 뒤 `ww_order` 군 명령이 CSK 군에 들어가고 POL 군은 그대로; `isHumanControlled(CSK)` 참, `isHumanControlled(POL)` 거짓; CSK의 P1 AI가 다음 차례에 돌지 않음), "the vanilla rules are untouched when the mode is off"(옵션 끔 판 300틱의 해시가 P5 전 고정값과 같음 — `VanillaDigest`의 값을 그대로 쓴다).
- `WarRecords.test.ts`: "a declaration creates a war with both sides"(DEU → POL: 전쟁 1, 편 [DEU] / [POL]), "faction members pulled in by a declaration join the same war"(DEU → FRA: FRA의 진영 GBR이 같은 전쟁 방어 편), "a join enters the target's oldest war on the other side"(DEU → POL, DEU → CSK(전쟁 2), SUN `joinWar` DEU → 전쟁 1의 방어 편), "every pair belongs to exactly one war (1,000 random declarations)"(시드 고정 무작위 선포·참전·강화 1,000번 뒤 `pairWar` 열쇠 = `WorldWar`의 쌍 집합, 쌍마다 그 전쟁의 두 편에 한쪽씩), "end_war marks a member left when it has no pair in that war", "a faction joining mid-war fights the other side".

- `CoreTable.test.ts`: "1936 cores come from the scenario and CORES_1936"(주 15·20은 CSK와 SVK의 코어), "a fourth core pushes out the smallest gone holder", "setLegalOwner with core adds the receiver", "administered = controller without a core and someone else with one"(DEU가 15를 통제 → 관리; DEU가 4 Munich(DEU 코어)를 통제 → 아님; 코어 없는 주(시험에서 `munich_test`의 코어를 지움)를 SUN이 통제 → 아님).
